Abstract: Optimal decision trees (ODTs) are compact, interpretable machine learning models that globally optimize a given objective, but their scalability remains challenging. While recent work has proposed a variety of search strategies to improve scalability, the precise contribution of each strategy remains unclear. To address this gap, we introduce a general algorithmic framework for ODTs that instantiates previously used search strategies and enables the definition of new ones. This provides a common lens through which to understand and compare different strategies, which we use to empirically investigate the effect of 18 search strategies. Compared to the state of the art, the best strategy in our evaluation achieves significantly better anytime performance for classification, and improves runtime by more than an order of magnitude for regression.

</details>


### [94] [Multi-channel Uplift Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28182)
*Changjian Liu,Tianyu Wang,Xiaoxuan Deng,WenTao Zhu,Yuwei Xu,Jungqi Jin,Yong Gao,Chuan Yu,Jian Xu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReAlloc，一个用于电商平台多渠道营销预算分配的快速-慢速因果框架，通过正交教师网络提取无偏局部梯度、解释引导学生网络蒸馏为长期边际场，实现支持感知的保守决策，在淘宝平台的模拟和大规模A/B测试中同时提升了支付订单量和收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台需要在多个渠道间分配固定营销预算以最大化业务效用，但标准的预测-优化（PTO）范式在组合空间中因观测混杂和严重外推而失效，现有方法难以处理跨渠道替代效应和预算约束下的决策问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为单纯形约束的提升决策问题，提出ReAlloc快速-慢速因果框架：敏捷的正交教师（Orthogonal Teacher）从短期日志中提取无偏局部梯度，解释引导学生（Explanation-Guided Student）将其蒸馏为长期视野上的结构化边际场，从而实现支持感知的保守决策并捕捉跨渠道替代效应。

Result: 在淘宝平台上进行了广泛的模拟实验和大规模在线A/B测试，ReAlloc在支付订单量和收入两个指标上均实现了同步提升。

Conclusion: ReAlloc框架有效解决了电商多渠道预算分配中PTO范式在组合空间中失效的问题，通过因果驱动的快速-慢速知识蒸馏实现了支持感知的保守决策，在实际工业场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: E-commerce platforms must allocate fixed marketing budgets across multiple channels to maximize business utility. However, standard predict-then-optimize (PTO) paradigms fail in this compositional space due to observational confounding and severe extrapolation. We formulate this challenge as a simplex-constrained uplift decision problem and propose ReAlloc, a fast-slow causal framework. Specifically, an agile Orthogonal Teacher extracts unbiased local gradients from short-term logs, while an Explanation-Guided Student distills them into a structured marginal field over long-term horizons. This design enables support-aware, conservative decisions that capture cross-channel substitutions. Extensive simulations and large-scale online A/B tests on Taobao platform demonstrate that ReAlloc achieves simultaneous lifts in both pay order and income.

</details>


### [95] [Persistent Gaussian Perturbations Prevent Oversmoothing in Recurrent Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28185)
*Mostafa Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了向循环图神经网络中注入高斯噪声来防止过度平滑（oversmoothing）的机制。在确定性更新的全局收缩假设下，将带噪声的架构建模为随机动力系统，证明了隐表示构成几何遍历的马尔可夫链，并推导出稳态Dirichlet能量的正下界，该下界与噪声方差和图谱间隙成正比，从而严格保证表示不会坍缩到常数流形上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图神经网络存在过度平滑的根本性限制，即重复的消息传递导致节点表示趋于相似并坍缩到低维子空间，这限制了消息传递架构的有效深度，需要寻找保持表示多样性的机制。

Method: 将注入独立高斯噪声的循环GNN建模为随机动力系统；在全局收缩假设下证明隐表示构成几何遍历的马尔可夫链；推导稳态期望Dirichlet能量的显式正下界；通过线性和非线性循环GNN的数值实验验证理论预测。

Result: 建立了稳态Dirichlet能量的正下界，与噪声方差和图谱间隙成正比；证明了稳态表示不会坍缩到常数流形，即渐进过度平滑被严格防止；数值实验与理论预测高度吻合，展示了平稳分布的出现以及极限Dirichlet能量对噪声强度的依赖性。

Conclusion: 持续随机扰动是抵抗过度平滑的一种根本不同机制，补充了现有的基于残差连接、归一化和图重连等确定性方法，为设计更深层的图神经网络提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Oversmoothing is a fundamental limitation of deep graph neural networks (GNNs), where repeated message passing causes node representations to become increasingly similar, eventually collapsing toward a low-dimensional subspace. This phenomenon limits the effective depth of message-passing architectures and motivates the search for mechanisms that preserve representation diversity. In this paper, we study a recurrent graph neural network in which independent Gaussian noise is injected after every propagation step and analyze the resulting architecture as a stochastic dynamical system. Under a standard global contraction assumption on the deterministic update, we prove that the hidden representations form a geometrically ergodic Markov chain admitting a unique invariant probability measure. Our main theoretical result establishes an explicit positive lower bound on the expected stationary Dirichlet energy, proportional to both the noise variance and the spectral gap of the underlying graph. Consequently, the stationary representations cannot collapse onto the constant manifold, providing a rigorous guarantee that asymptotic oversmoothing is prevented in the sense of non-vanishing Dirichlet energy. Our analysis reveals persistent stochastic perturbations as a fundamentally different mechanism for combating oversmoothing, complementing existing deterministic approaches based on residual connections, normalization, and graph rewiring. Finally, numerical experiments on both linear and nonlinear recurrent graph neural networks closely match the theoretical predictions, illustrating the emergence of a stationary distribution and the predicted dependence of the limiting Dirichlet energy on the noise intensity.

</details>


### [96] [TopoFormer: Topology Meets Attention for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28259)
*Md Joshem Uddin,Astrit Tola,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Topoformer，一个轻量级且可扩展的图表示学习框架，通过Topo-Scan模块将图的拓扑结构编码为注意力友好的序列，利用Transformer生成图级嵌入，在保持理论稳定性保证的同时，在图分类和分子性质预测基准上达到或超越现有SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持久同调方法虽然能捕捉图的多尺度拓扑结构，但计算代价高昂且难以与深度学习架构无缝集成。作者旨在开发一种既保留拓扑归纳偏置，又具有可并行性和高效性的图表示学习方法。

Method: Topoformer包含核心模块Topo-Scan，通过沿节点或边过滤进行切片，将图分解为短的有序拓扑令牌序列，捕捉从局部模体到全局组织的多尺度结构模式，然后由Transformer处理生成图级嵌入。该模块可并行化，避免了昂贵的持久图（diagram）计算。

Result: 方法在拓扑编码稳定性上提供了理论保证，并在图分类和分子性质预测基准上达到了SOTA性能，匹配或超过强GNN和基于拓扑的基线模型，同时计算效率可预测且高效。

Conclusion: 这项工作为图表示学习开辟了并行化、统一的新路径，成功将拓扑归纳偏置集成到注意力框架中，为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We introduce Topoformer, a lightweight and scalable framework for graph representation learning that encodes topological structure into attention-friendly sequences. At the core of our method is Topo-Scan, a novel module that decomposes a graph into a short, ordered sequence of topological tokens by slicing over node or edge filtrations. These sequences capture multi-scale structural patterns, from local motifs to global organization, and are processed by a Transformer to produce expressive graph-level embeddings. Unlike traditional persistent homology pipelines, Topo-Scan is parallelizable, avoids costly diagram computations, and integrates seamlessly with standard deep learning architectures. We provide theoretical guarantees on the stability of our topological encodings and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across graph classification and molecular property prediction benchmarks. Our results show that Topoformer matches or exceeds strong GNN and topology-based baselines while offering predictable and efficient compute. This work opens a new path for parallelizable and unifying approaches to graph representation learning that integrate topological inductive biases into attention frameworks.

</details>


### [97] [HARGO: Heterogeneity-Aware Reward-Guided Optimization for RL Post-Training of LLMs on HPC Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28301)
*Tiangang Li,Xiangbo Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARGO是一种面向高绩效计算任务（HPC）的异构感知奖励引导优化方法，通过置信度调制的优势加权机制，在不依赖任务类型标签的情况下，有效提升了大语言模型在异构HPC任务上的对齐质量，在四项任务和九种方法中均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）虽然能为大语言模型注入HPC领域知识，但知识本身并不能保证任务行为恰当——例如同一模型在数据竞争检测上表现良好，却在事实问答中产生冗长且不精确的回答。HPC任务具有极端异构性（二进制分类、事实QA和语义生成的答案长度差异达58倍，跨越三种不同的奖励分布），使得统一权重的强化学习方法（如GRPO）表现次优。

Method: HARGO提出基于每条响应重要性加权的机制，通过两个关键信号实现：1）从分组级奖励对比中计算判别（discrimination）信号；2）从参考模型对数概率中计算置信度（confidence）信号。将这两个信号调制优势值后计算每条响应的权重，整个过程无需任务类型标签。

Result: 在四项HPC任务和九种方法的对比中，HARGO在所有三个主要指标上均取得最佳性能：WinRate为54.62%，Data Race F1为91.30%，PLP相似度为0.8558。消融实验证实了两个信号具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: HARGO在异构HPC任务中建立了最佳的整体对齐质量，证明了异构感知的奖励引导优化方法的有效性。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can equip large language models (LLMs) with domain knowledge for high-performance computing (HPC) tasks such as data race detection and benchmark question answering. However, knowledge alone does not guarantee task-appropriate behavior: the same SFT model that correctly classifies 88.65\% of C/C++ data race samples produces verbose, imprecise answers to factual queries, with 65.9\% of MLPerf responses exceeding 40 characters. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training addresses this gap by optimizing for task-specific rewards rather than token-level imitation. Yet HPC tasks exhibit extreme heterogeneity, with binary classification, factual QA, and semantic generation differing by 58x in answer length, spanning three distinct reward distributions, and showing widely varying SFT accuracy. This makes uniform-weight RL methods such as GRPO suboptimal. We propose HARGO, Heterogeneity-Aware Reward-Guided Optimization, which introduces per-response importance weighting via confidence-modulated advantage: computing a discrimination signal from group-level reward contrast and a confidence signal from reference model log-probabilities, then modulating the advantage before computing per-response weights, without requiring task-type labels. Across four HPC tasks and nine methods, HARGO achieves the best performance on all three primary metrics: WinRate 54.62\%, Data Race F1 91.30\%, and PLP Similarity 0.8558. Ablation confirms complementary contributions from both signals. HARGO establishes the best overall alignment quality among compared methods for heterogeneous HPC tasks.

</details>


### [98] [Semi-Supervised Learning for Molecular Graphs via Ensemble Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28304)
*Rasmus Tirsgaard,Laurits Fredsgaard,Marisa Wodrich,Mikkel Jordahn,Mikkel N. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用集成共识的半监督学习方法，无需标签保持的数据增强，即可在分子数据集上显著提升图神经网络（GNN）的预测准确率、鲁棒性和校准性能，单个集成成员甚至优于传统全集成监督训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子科学中标注数据获取成本高昂，而未标注的分子数据大量可得。然而标准半监督方法依赖标签保持的增强策略，这在分子领域难以设计——微小的结构变化可能剧烈改变性质。因此需要一种不依赖此类增强的半监督方法。

Method: 利用集成共识（ensemble consensus）作为半监督训练目标，在多个分子数据集、任务类型和图神经网络架构上进行训练，不依赖标签保持增强，通过模型之间的共识来利用未标注数据。

Result: 该方法在多种分子数据集、任务类型和GNN架构上均能提升预测准确率；训练后模型鲁棒性增强；单个集成成员的性能在几乎所有情况下优于传统监督方式训练的全集成；同时半监督训练降低了校准误差，表现出类似知识蒸馏的效果。

Conclusion: 基于集成共识的半监督学习是分子科学中一种有效且通用的策略，能够规避标签保持增强的设计难题，同时带来性能、鲁棒性和校准方面的多重收益。

Abstract: Machine learning is transforming molecular sciences by accelerating property prediction, simulation, and the discovery of new molecules and materials. Acquiring labeled data in these domains is often costly and time-consuming, whereas large collections of unlabeled molecular data are readily available. Standard semi-supervised learning methods often rely on label-preserving augmentations, which are challenging to design in the molecular domain, where minor changes can drastically alter properties. In this work, we show that semi-supervised methods that rely on an ensemble consensus can boost predictive accuracy across a diverse range of molecular datasets, task types, and graph neural network architectures. We find that training with an ensemble consensus objective increases robustness in models and exhibits an effect similar to knowledge distillation; an individual member of an ensemble trained this way outperforms a full ensemble trained in a traditional supervised fashion in almost all cases. In addition, this type of semi-supervised training reduces calibration error.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond Geometric Complementarity: Coherent Overlap in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28308)
*Huiyuan Tian,Bonan Xu,Shijian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入专家子空间分离指数（ESSI）、匹配路由残差和前缀控制的2×2析因设计，将稀疏MoE中路由共选专家、候选质量和候选-上下文交互这三个量区分开，提出"相干重叠"（coherent overlap）概念来解释为什么几何相似性本身不能决定冗余性或剪枝价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE语言模型将每个token路由到多个专家，其收益被认为源于共同选中的专家应贡献不同的表示方向（几何解释）。然而现有证据往往混淆了路由连贯性、候选质量和候选-上下文交互这三者，难以真正厘清几何相似性如何影响MoE的实际功能价值。

Method: 提出三种互补的量化工具：(1) 专家子空间分离指数（ESSI）度量专家子空间重叠；(2) 匹配路由残差比较实际路由与匹配替代方案的解释力；(3) 前缀控制的2×2析因设计区分候选质量与候选-上下文交互。同时使用冻结路由干预和受控Top-k训练研究评估功能价值。

Result: 六个MoE架构中专家子空间显著重叠，但实际路由比匹配替代方案更好地解释token表示；在39个析因单元中，被选候选在所有单元中都比最强未选候选解释更多残差，但实际前缀在所有交互中均为负且95%置信区间低于零；冻结路由比较中24/39个案例增加后期专家提升下一token预测，受控训练研究中Top-2在三个种子中均优于Top-1。

Conclusion: 提出"相干重叠"（coherent overlap）这一联合模式：路由从共享几何邻域中选出token相关专家，但有用的多专家计算仍然存在而无需不相交的线性覆盖。这些量化分离澄清了为何几何相似性本身不能单独决定冗余性或剪枝价值。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models route each token to multiple experts, suggesting a geometric account of their benefit: co-selected experts should contribute distinct representation directions. Existing evidence often conflates route coherence, candidate quality, and candidate-by-context interaction. We distinguish these quantities using an Expert Subspace Separation Index (ESSI), matched-route residuals, and a prefix-controlled $2\times2$ factorial; frozen-route interventions and a controlled Top-$k$ study assess functional value. Three paired contrasts organize the findings. First, across six MoE architectures, expert subspaces overlap substantially, yet actual routes explain token representations better than matched alternatives. Second, across the 39 factorial cells in OLMoE, Mixtral, and DeepSeek, the selected candidate explains more of the residual representation than the strongest unselected rival in every cell, yet the actual prefix narrows this advantage throughout: all interactions are negative, and every 95% confidence interval lies below zero. Third, this geometric narrowing does not imply functional redundancy: adding later experts improves next-token prediction in 24 of 39 frozen-route comparisons, while the other 15 estimates are inconclusive; a controlled training study also favors Top-2 over Top-1 in all three seeds. We call this joint pattern coherent overlap: routing selects token-relevant experts from a shared geometric neighborhood, while useful multi-expert computation persists without disjoint linear coverage. Separating these quantities clarifies why geometric similarity alone cannot determine redundancy or pruning value.

</details>


### [100] [Measuring Distortion in the Empty Regions of Dimensionality Reduction Scatterplots with the Gap Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28324)
*Jaume Ros,Alessio Arleo,Fernando Paulovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的二维投影质量度量方法——Gap Index (GI)，通过衡量投影中空白区域的空间变形来捕捉视觉失真，弥补了传统质量指标忽略空白区域失真的不足，且计算快速、结果可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流质量度量指标（如距离或邻域关系）主要关注点与点之间的直接关系，却忽略了投影布局中空白区域的失真。然而这些空白区域往往构成了二维布局中视觉上显著的特征，因此需要一种能捕捉空白区域视觉失真的度量方法。

Method: 将投影空间分解为空三角形（empty triangles），然后将这些三角形与其在高维空间中的对应物进行比较来计算形变程度。每个三角形的形变可以聚合为一个标量值，也可以叠加在投影上以可视化区域失真的分布模式。

Result: 与主流质量指标相比，GI对具有高视觉影响的小型结构变形更为敏感，同时计算速度快且结果易于解释。

Conclusion: Gap Index是一种有效的二维投影质量度量方法，能够补充现有指标的不足，捕捉空白区域的视觉失真，为高维数据投影的可视化分析提供更全面的质量评估工具。

Abstract: Quality metrics play a crucial role in the proper use of dimensionality reduction projections for visual analysis of high-dimensional data. They quantify the degree of distortion of a projection compared to the high-dimensional data and provide a reliable indication of how confident users can be in the structures they see in the resulting layouts. However, most popular metrics focus on capturing direct relationships between points (e.g., distances or neighborhoods) while neglecting distortions in empty areas of the layout, even though these often compose visually relevant features of a 2D layout. In this paper, we introduce the Gap Index (GI), a quality metric for 2D projections that captures visual distortion by measuring spatial distortion in empty areas of a projection. It does so by decomposing the space into empty triangles, which are then compared to their high-dimensional counterparts to compute the deformation. This per-triangle deformation can be aggregated into a single scalar value or overlaid on a projection to visualize regional distortion patterns. Results show that, contrary to popular quality metrics, the GI is sensitive to small structural deformations that have high visual impact. It is also fast to compute and interpretable.

</details>


### [101] [LEDGERMIND: Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28374)
*Enjun Du,Hange Zhou,Chenxu Du,Siyi Liu,Zirong Chen,Ziyu Zheng,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LedgerMind框架，将多模态智能体轨迹建模为受来源约束的状态机，通过结构化证据账本、三层接地协议、自适应双路径调度器和带证明非放大保证的验证修复引擎，在提升答案准确率的同时改善轨迹级忠实度，针对性地解决四项被最终准确率掩盖的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态智能体评估仅依赖最终答案准确率，无法区分正确答案是通过有根据的证据、语言先验还是误差抵消得到的，最终准确率掩盖了中间推理中的关键缺陷。

Method: 1) 将工具输出规范化为结构化证据账本作为轨迹状态；2) 下游推理只能引用活跃账本条目，在实体和数值层面检查接地性；3) 修复实现为类型化状态转换且不能引入无工具来源的内容；4) 配套三层接地协议、自适应双路径调度器（按问题复杂度匹配推理深度）和事件触发验证修复引擎，并给出形式化来源非放大保证。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准和不同骨干MLLM上的实验表明，LedgerMind同时提升了答案准确率和轨迹级忠实度。

Conclusion: 通过来源约束的轨迹建模，可以有效解决最终答案准确率所掩盖的四类失败模式（无依据的中间推理、引用式实体幻觉、简单问题的过度推理和修复时放大），实现更可靠的视觉问答智能体。

Abstract: Multimodal agents for visual question answering increasingly operate as multi-step trajectories that interleave perception, retrieval, and reasoning, yet evaluation still largely reduces to final-answer accuracy. This aggregate signal cannot tell whether a correct answer was reached through grounded evidence, language priors, or accidental error cancellation. We propose to treat a multimodal agent trajectory as a provenance-constrained state machine: tool outputs are normalized into a Structured Evidence Ledger that serves as the trajectory state, downstream reasoning and decision claims may cite only active ledger entries, grounding is checked at the entity and numeric level, and repair is realized as typed state transitions that cannot introduce content without tool-produced provenance. We instantiate this design as LedgerMind (Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger), augmented by a Three-Layer Grounding Protocol, an Adaptive Dual-Path Dispatcher that matches reasoning depth to question complexity, and an Event-Triggered Verification-and-Repair engine with a formal provenance non-amplification guarantee. We use LedgerMind to target four recurring failure patterns that final-answer accuracy tends to obscure: unsupported intermediate reasoning, citation-backed entity hallucination (Phantom Grounding), over-reasoning on simple queries, and repair-time amplification. Experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks and backbone MLLMs show that LedgerMind improves both answer accuracy and trajectory-level faithfulness.

</details>


### [102] [Hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo for Order-Optimal Neural Actor-Critic in Average-Reward CMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28390)
*Ankur Naskar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分层多层蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家的原始-对偶自然Actor-Critic算法，首次在无限时域平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中，使用神经评论家和一般策略参数化实现了$\tilde{O}(T^{-1/2})$阶的最优收敛保证，同时无需知道底层混合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMDP是安全关键强化学习的自然框架，但现有原始-对偶actor-critic方法仅在线性评论家下具有阶最优收敛保证。神经评论家在NTK分析下存在根本性的偏差-成本权衡问题：降低评论家偏差会大幅增加优化成本，阻碍阶最优收敛的达成，这是一个尚未解决的关键瓶颈。

Method: 引入分层多层蒙特卡洛（Multilevel Monte Carlo, MLMC）神经评论家，在轨迹采样和评论家优化两个维度同时进行去偏。该方法能以对数期望样本成本达到长评论家优化运行的偏差水平，并在此基础上构建原始-对偶Natural Actor-Critic算法。

Result: 所提出的算法实现了最优性差距和约束违反均为$\tilde{O}(T^{-1/2})$阶，建立了无限时域平均奖励CMDPs中首个使用一般策略参数化和神经评论家的阶最优收敛保证，且无需知道混合时间。该结果在无约束设置下同样具有新颖性。

Conclusion: 本文通过MLMC神经评论家解决了神经critic估计中偏差-成本权衡的根本瓶颈，首次将阶最优收敛保证推广到具有神经评论家的平均奖励CMDP场景，为该领域确立了新的理论基准。

Abstract: Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) provide a natural framework for reinforcement learning in safety-critical applications, where agents maximize long-term reward while satisfying long-term constraints. Although primal-dual actor-critic methods with linear critics are well understood, extending order-optimal convergence guarantees to neural critics in average-reward CMDPs has remained open. The main challenge is a fundamental bias-cost trade-off in neural critic estimation: under Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) analysis, reducing critic bias substantially increases critic optimization cost, preventing order-optimal convergence in the primal-dual framework. We resolve this bottleneck by introducing a hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) neural critic that performs debiasing simultaneously across trajectory sampling and critic optimization. The resulting estimator attains the bias of a long critic optimization run with only logarithmic expected sample cost. Building on this estimator, we develop a primal-dual Natural Actor-Critic algorithm that achieves both an optimality gap and a constraint violation of order $\tilde{O}(T^{-1/2})$. This establishes the first order-optimal convergence guarantees for infinite-horizon average-reward CMDPs with general policy parameterization and neural critics, while eliminating the need to know the underlying mixing time. Our results are novel even in the unconstrained setting.

</details>


### [103] [Why Are GUI Agents Correct but Late? Decode on the Decision-Time Critical Path, Tested with Pre-Compiled Policy Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28399)
*Zihan Dong,Rui Qian,Qishi Zhan,Dongshen Peng,Kaixin Li,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应预期策略树（AAPT），通过在空闲屏幕时段预构建有界条件策略树来消除多模态模型在决策关键路径上的自回归解码延迟。实验表明AAPT在争议决策窗口内将成功率从0.50提升至0.79（p=1.8×10⁻³），且未产生错误动作；在独立模型上以126对配对试验复现效果（p=4.9×10⁻¹³）。AAPT适用于可预先枚举候选动作的场景，而反应式执行在无法枚举时依然更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体常因瞬时GUI事件失败，因为其产生正确动作时相关窗口已关闭。根本原因是昂贵的自回归解码位于决策关键路径上，导致响应延迟。

Method: AAPT在不修改底层模型的前提下，利用空闲屏幕时段让同一冻结多模态模型构建有界条件策略树，包含可观察守卫、预授权动作和分支特定截止时间。树的大小覆盖模型自身解码延迟。事件发生时，轻量级观察器将变更门控帧与预准备分支匹配，立即执行对应动作而无需生成新文本。

Result: 配对试验中AAPT将成功率从0.50提升至0.79（p=1.8×10⁻³），无错误动作产生；开放循环和预测-重规划基线因仍在执行时解码而成功率为零。在独立通用多模态模型上以126对配对试验复现（p=4.9×10⁻¹³）。消融实验揭示三个关键要求：快速观察器解码、有效树规划、准确分支路由。预注册Oracle探针否定了初始假设，指出分支路由是因果瓶颈。外部基准上AAPT整体性能与反应式基线相当，但两者优势互补。

Conclusion: AAPT在可预先枚举候选动作的场景中表现最佳，而反应式执行在无法枚举时依然更强。这为计算机使用智能体处理瞬时GUI事件提供了新的延迟规避方案。

Abstract: Computer-use agents often fail on transient GUI events because they produce the correct action only after the relevant window has already closed. We identify the main cause as expensive autoregressive decoding on the decision-time critical path. We propose Adaptive Anticipatory Policy Trees (AAPT), which eliminates this delay without modifying the underlying model. During idle screen periods, the same frozen multimodal model constructs a bounded conditional policy tree with observable guards, pre-authorized actions, and branch-specific deadlines. The tree is sized to cover the model's own decoding latency. When an event occurs, a lightweight observer matches change-gated frames to a prepared branch and immediately executes the corresponding action without generating new text. In paired trials with pre-registered endpoints and exact McNemar tests, AAPT improves the success rate from 0.50 to 0.79 within a contested decision window ($p=1.8\times10^{-3}$), while producing no incorrect actions. Both open-loop and predict-and-replan baselines achieve zero success because they still decode during execution. A preparation-time sweep shows that the gain emerges where the latency-based tree-sizing rule predicts, and ablations reveal three key requirements: fast observer decoding, valid tree planning, and accurate branch routing. A pre-registered oracle probe rejects our initial hypothesis and instead points to branch routing as the causal bottleneck. We further reproduce the effect on an independent general-purpose multimodal model over 126 paired trials ($p=4.9\times10^{-13}$). On an external benchmark, AAPT matches the overall performance of a reactive baseline, although the two methods exhibit complementary strengths. Together, these results suggest that AAPT performs best when candidate actions can be enumerated in advance, whereas reactive execution remains stronger when they cannot.

</details>


### [104] [On-Policy and Off-Policy Learning for Large Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28408)
*Imad Aouali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模动作空间下的上下文多臂老虎机策略学习，涵盖在线学习（遗憾最小化）和离线学习（基于日志数据）两种范式，提出了结构化贝叶斯方法（meTS和dTS）用于在线学习，以及结构化直接方法（sDM）、凹优化目标和可微悲观方法用于离线学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模动作空间中，上下文多臂老虎机面临探索效率低下、数据覆盖稀疏、重要性权重高方差、外推偏差大以及优化景观困难等重大挑战，现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: 在线学习方面提出meTS（混合效应汤普森采样扩展）和dTS（利用扩散启发式先验建模动作间依赖关系）；离线学习方面提出基于潜变量的结构化直接方法sDM，以及基于指数平滑和PAC-Bayesian界的可微悲观方法，并引入凹的可高效优化的策略加权对数似然目标。

Result: 所提出的方法能够在动作间共享信息，遗憾保证依赖于有效动作数而非全部动作数；实验（或理论分析）表明在大型动作空间下优化误差可能主导估计误差，而新提出的凹目标函数可高效优化。

Conclusion: 通过结构化建模和方法创新，本文系统地解决了大规模动作空间下在线和离线策略学习的核心挑战，为实际应用中的高效决策提供了理论基础和算法保障。

Abstract: This thesis studies policy learning in interactive systems where an agent observes a context, selects an action from a very large set, and receives partial feedback. The main framework is contextual bandits, with two paradigms: on-policy learning, where the agent interacts sequentially with the environment and minimizes regret, and off-policy learning, where it learns from logged data collected by a logging policy. In large action spaces, both settings face major challenges: inefficient exploration, sparse data coverage, high-variance importance weights, extrapolation bias, and difficult optimization landscapes. The first part develops structured Bayesian methods for on-policy learning. We introduce meTS, a mixed-effect extension of Thompson sampling, and dTS, which leverages diffusion-inspired priors to model dependencies between actions. These methods share information across actions and yield regret guarantees depending on an effective number of actions. The second part addresses off-policy learning. We propose sDM, a structured direct method based on latent variables, show that optimization error can dominate estimation error in large action spaces, and introduce concave, efficiently optimizable policy-weighted log-likelihood objectives. Finally, we develop differentiable pessimistic methods based on exponential smoothing and PAC-Bayesian bounds to control the bias-variance trade-off of regularized importance-sampling estimators.

</details>


### [105] [QAdapt: A Noise-Adaptive Neural Pre-Decoding Framework for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28422)
*Ran Miao,Rui Luo,Xiaohan Shan,Xiaoming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAdapt是一个噪声自适应的神经预解码框架，用于表面码量子纠错。它通过捕获综合征数据中的局部时空相关性，顺序适应演化的噪声条件并缓解灾难性遗忘，将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器。在110种合成分布外噪声配置上持续降低逻辑错误率，并在谷歌Willow基准数据上实现高达5.79%的逻辑错误率降低和9.32%的后端解码延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算的性能不仅受物理噪声限制，还受经典解码器处理快速生成综合征数据的延迟限制。硬件噪声具有强、异构、非平稳的特点，且模拟到硬件的分布偏移会显著降低固定神经解码器的性能，因此需要能够适应演化噪声条件的自适应解码框架。

Method: QAdapt框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 捕获综合征数据中局部时空相关性的神经子网络；(2) 顺序自适应机制，能够随噪声条件演化而更新，同时通过缓解灾难性遗忘来保持先前学到的知识；(3) 将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器的接口设计，实现与现有解码系统的兼容。

Result: 在110种合成分布外噪声配置下，QAdapt始终优于神经预解码基线。在谷歌Willow基准数据上无需目标域微调，实现逻辑错误率最高降低5.79%，后端解码延迟最高降低9.32%。

Conclusion: QAdapt提供了一种实用且与解码器兼容的方法，能够在演化的硬件噪声条件下提升量子纠错的鲁棒性和后端解码效率。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) relies on quantum error correction to suppress physical errors and preserve logical information at scale. In practice, however, performance is constrained not only by physical noise but also by the latency of classical decoders processing rapidly generated syndrome data. This challenge is exacerbated by hardware noise that is strong, heterogeneous, and nonstationary, as well as by the simulation-to-hardware distribution shift that can substantially degrade fixed neural decoders. We present QAdapt, a noise-adaptive neural pre-decoding framework for surface-code quantum error correction. QAdapt captures local spatiotemporal correlations in syndrome data, sequentially adapts to evolving noise conditions while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and forwards the residual syndrome to a conventional global decoder. Across 110 synthetic out-of-distribution noise configurations for rotated surface-code memory circuits, QAdapt consistently reduces the logical error rate relative to the neural pre-decoding baseline. On Google's Willow benchmark data, without target-domain fine-tuning, it achieves reductions of up to 5.79 percent in logical error rate and 9.32 percent in backend decoding latency on the residual syndrome. These results demonstrate that QAdapt provides a practical and decoder-compatible approach to improving the robustness and backend decoding efficiency of quantum error correction under evolving hardware noise.

</details>


### [106] [Kohn-Sham Spectral Embedding on Sparse Graphs at the Nishimori Temperature for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28428)
*V. S. Usatyuk,D. A. Sapozhnikov,S. I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kohn-Sham谱嵌入（KSSE），一种物理启发的能量基模型，用稀疏图谱嵌入替代稠密CNN分类器。通过将预训练特征映射到准循环LDPC图，构造Kohn-Sham哈密顿量的正则化拉普拉斯算子，利用FFT在O(N log N + k²_mode N)时间内求解D个独立通道谱问题。在ImageNet-1000上以约21.24M参数达到88.93% Top-1准确率，超越Swin-L且匹配ViT-H/14，模型体积分别减少10倍和30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的稠密CNN分类器参数冗余且计算开销大，作者希望构建一个物理启发的能量基模型，通过稀疏图谱嵌入实现高效分类，在保持甚至超越大规模Transformer模型性能的同时大幅减少模型参数。

Method: KSSE将预训练特征映射到准循环LDPC图，构造类似Kohn-Sham哈密顿量的正则化拉普拉斯算子；利用Pontryagin自对偶性通过FFT求解谱问题；提出星域手术（star-domain surgery）优化图拓扑，通过构造局部凸性保持信息码字；使用多重分形分析（D₂谱）验证从粗糙区到星域盆地的景观转变；证明六个理论结果，包括广义Ihara-Bass恒等式、陷阱集特征值对应、可加通道可分性等。

Result: 在ImageNet-1000上使用冻结EfficientNet-B4特征（D=1792），KSSE以约21.24M参数达到88.93% Top-1准确率，超越Swin-L（197M，86.4-87.3%）并匹配ViT-H/14（632M，88.0-89.5%）的归纳设置性能，模型体积分别减少10倍和30倍。

Conclusion: KSSE证明了物理启发的稀疏图谱方法可以替代大规模稠密模型，在极小参数量下达到顶尖性能，展示了图拓扑优化与谱方法结合的强大潜力，为高效深度学习模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Kohn--Sham Spectral Embedding (KSSE), a physics-inspired energy-based model replacing dense CNN classifiers with a sparse-graph spectral embedding evaluated at the Nishimori temperature of an associated Random-Bond Ising Model. By mapping pre-trained features onto quasi-cyclic low-density parity-check graphs and constructing a regularized Laplacian acting as a Kohn--Sham Hamiltonian, we solve $D$ independent channel spectral problems in $\mathcal{O}(N\log N + k^2_{\text{mode}} N)$ time via FFT on circulant blocks (leveraging Pontryagin self-duality of $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$) and low-order Rayleigh refinement. Graph topology is optimized using \emph{star-domain surgery}: rather than destroying information-carrying codewords by removing frustrated cycles, we construct edge shifts creating local convexity around codewords while bounding residual frustration to $ρ(B_γ)\leq 1+δ$. Multi-scale fractal analysis ($D_2$ spectrum) and fractal learning-rate landscape certifies a landscape transition from rough regimes ($D_2>3$) to star-domain basins ($D_2<1$), enabling Rayleigh refinement with $k_{\text{mode}}=5$ modes. We prove six theoretical results: a generalized Ihara--Bass identity linking belief propagation to the Laplacian; trapping-set eigenvalue correspondence; additive channel separability with an explicit exchange-correlation bound; a surgery theorem bounding frustration with attractor width $Ω(1/\sqrt{d_{\min}})$; a quasi-stationarity perturbation bound; and a fixed-point convergence theorem. In a transductive protocol on ImageNet-1000 with frozen EfficientNet-B4 features ($D=1792$), KSSE achieves \textbf{88.93\%} Top-1 accuracy using $\approx 21.24$M parameters, outperforming Swin-L (197M, 86.4--87.3\%) and matching ViT-H/14 (632M, 88.0--89.5\%) under standard inductive setups, while reducing model footprint by $10\times$ and $30\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [107] [Oracle-Budgeted Molecular Optimization with Short-Term Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28437)
*Jiannan Yang,Veronika Thost,Xiang Ling,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"短期图记忆"的即插即用模块，通过在线图神经网络代理对候选分子进行预筛选，使有限的oracle预算更专注于高预测效用的分子，从而在不增加oracle调用次数的前提下提升分子优化的top-10得分，且在四种生成器上均表现稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化通常在有限的oracle预算下进行，这使得决定评估哪些分子与决定生成哪些分子同等重要。现有方法忽视了如何更有效地分配有限的oracle查询预算这一关键问题。

Method: 提出短期图记忆模块，它是一个即插即用组件，保持原有生成器架构和原生更新规则不变，同时利用先前评估过的分子进行学习。该模块维护一个在线图神经网络代理，对每轮候选池进行预筛选，确保有限的oracle预算被用于预测效用更高的分子。

Result: 在标准分子优化基准上，该方法在片段生成器上的平均top-10得分有提升，且不增加oracle成本，在任意oracle上均未落后于基线。在1000次调用的紧凑预算下，四种测试的生成器均获得收益。分析表明其收益与生成器的搜索广度和原生搜索对oracle反馈的利用效率有关。

Conclusion: 提供了一种简单有效的方式，使固定oracle预算的选择性更强，并给出了哪些生成器能从中受益的证据，为有限预算下的分子优化提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Molecular optimization is commonly performed under a limited oracle budget, which makes deciding what to evaluate as important as deciding what to generate. We introduce short-term graph memory, a plug-in module that preserves the generator architecture and native update rule while learning from previously evaluated molecules to prioritize subsequent oracle queries. The module maintains an online graph neural surrogate that pre-screens each round's candidate pool, so the fixed oracle budget is spent on molecules with higher predicted utility. Applied to a fragment-based generator on a standard molecular optimization benchmark, it improves the mean top-10 score at no extra oracle cost and never falls behind the base on any oracle; the gain extends to all four generators we tested at a tight budget of one thousand calls. We then analyze how surrogate-guided selection interacts with the exploration and exploitation behavior of different generators. Its benefit at larger budgets is consistent with two properties of the backbone: how broadly it searches, and how effectively its native search already exploits oracle feedback. We provide a simple way to spend a fixed oracle budget more selectively, and evidence on which generators benefit from it.

</details>


### [108] [Cybersecurity Detection Classification with Reasoning-enabled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28460)
*Amol Khanna,Manu Nandan,Cristian Viorel Popa,Joan Pujol-Roig,Diana Bolocan,Laura Vasilie,Alexandru Apostu,Chase Helwig,Mihaela Gaman,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff,Chase Midler,Sven Krasser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对安全运营中心（SOC）中的警报疲劳问题，提出了一种结合思维链（CoT）推理、自动提示优化、自训练和可验证奖励强化学习的分类器系统。系统在真实Windows端点检测数据上达到82.6%的测试准确率，在高置信度操作点下，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%。此外，研究者发现CoT推理会降低标签token概率，因此单独训练了一个校准器来评估判定正确性，并证明该校准器是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临严重的警报疲劳问题——检测报告数量远超工作人员每日可处理的能力。以往工作直接让大语言模型（LLM）输出三分类标签，但没有训练模型对检测是否为真实威胁进行推理。作者希望训练模型不仅能给出标签，还能进行推理判断。

Method: 采用以下组合方法：1）自动提示优化（automated prompt optimization）；2）自训练（self-training）；3）带可验证奖励的强化学习（reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards）来训练一个具备思维链（CoT）推理能力的三分类器。由于CoT推理会降低标签token概率（影响自动分类的置信度判断），研究者还单独训练了一个校准器，读取完整的推理轨迹并估计判定正确的概率。

Result: 系统达到82.6%的测试准确率。在高置信度操作点下，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%（相对于直接标签LLM分类器）。研究表明：未训练的置信度判断器会导致高置信度召回率降为零，证明校准器训练的必要性；此外，微调的30B模型显著优于前沿通用模型，说明针对性训练比单纯扩大模型规模更有效。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了通过CoT推理训练三分类器的有效性，同时揭示了CoT推理对置信度评估的负面影响及校准器的重要性。研究还表明，对于特定的安全领域任务，定向微调比依赖大规模通用模型更具性价比。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [109] [Stage-Replay Divergence Follows the KV Cache: Fixed-Prefix Precision Controls and Bidirectional Cache Transplantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28495)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Qwen2.5系统中，在推理阶段边界处，使用保存的即时缓存（live cache）与单次预填充（fresh-prefill）重建token前缀对模型行为一致性的影响。实验发现BF16精度下两者会产生解码分歧，FP32下无分歧；K/V缓存是分歧轨迹的因果充分载体，数值精度调节其行为表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的"阶段重放诊断"方法假设可以从已到达前缀的原始解码器状态继续生成，而无需保留实时解码器状态。这一假设在推理系统诊断中未被充分审计，特别是跨整个推理阶段边界时，缓存保留与重新预填充是否等价值得严格检验。

Method: 采用200个匹配样本的实验设计：(1) 对比保留即时缓存与一次性预填充相同整数token；(2) 构建固定前缀2x2矩阵，交叉精度（BF16/FP32）与构造方式；(3) 提出"前向桥接"方法使逐token增量缓存与保留缓存位精确一致；(4) 对全部48层K/V键值层进行双向移植实验，验证缓存因果充分性。

Result: BF16下166个后缀和20个正确性标签存在差异，准确率仅差1点（95%CI [-3.5, +5.5]）；FP32无任何解码分歧（95% Wilson上界1.88%）。前向桥接在12/12行上达到位精确一致，全200行账本审计可复现所有轨迹。双向移植实验中，主检查点24/24和盲复制检查点43/43的差异续篇均跟随缓存供体。

Conclusion: 精确token重放可以重复，但不保证保留实时状态保真度。在测试状态下，边界K/V缓存是发散轨迹的因果充分载体，数值精度则调节其行为表达。这一发现为推理阶段诊断和缓存机制设计提供了重要实证依据。

Abstract: Stage-replay diagnostics reconstruct intermediate token prefixes and treat fresh-prefill continuation as continuation from the decoder state that originally reached the prefix. We audit that assumption at a whole reasoning-stage boundary in a Qwen2.5-derived system. A matched 200-item experiment compares retained live cache with one-shot prefill of identical integer tokens and places an exact replica on both sides. In BF16, replicas remain exact while the constructions differ on 166 suffixes and 20 correctness labels; the accuracy difference is only one point (paired 95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]). A fixed-prefix 2x2 holds all 200 token states constant while crossing construction and precision. The BF16 disagreements recur, whereas FP32 produces no decoded disagreement (95% Wilson upper bound 1.88%). A prospective bridge makes token-by-token incremental and retained live caches bit-exact on 12/12 rows; an all-200 saved-ledger audit reproduces every retained trajectory and comparison fingerprint. Bidirectional transplantation of all 48 key/value layers makes every tested divergent continuation follow its cache donor, both on a selected set at the primary checkpoint (24/24) and an outcome-blind replication at a later checkpoint (43/43). Exact-token replay can therefore be repeatable without preserving live-state fidelity. On the tested states, boundary K/V cache is a causally sufficient carrier of the divergent trajectory, while numerical precision moderates its behavioral expression.

</details>


### [110] [The Role of Causality in Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28497)
*Srikanth Avasarala,Varun Gupta,Shahin Jabbari,Saber Salehkaleybar,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果表现（causal performative）框架的算法救济（algorithmic recourse）方法，解决传统方法仅关注翻转预测结果而忽略真实资格提升的问题，通过因果关系建模来减少策略性博弈，实现稳定均衡并降低重复重训需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法救济方法只关注翻转模型预测，忽略了推荐改变是否真正提升个体资格或只是策略性博弈，导致部署后的救济策略引发行为响应、降低预测准确性，并在模型重训后失效。

Method: 提出因果表现框架，通过结构因果模型（SCM）建模救济动作在特征间的传播及其对真实标签的影响；分析非凸优化问题中表现稳定解的存在条件及高效计算方式，并证明因果救济可达到稳定均衡并减少博弈动机。

Result: 在半合成和真实信贷数据集上的实验表明，所提出的因果救济方法持续优于标准经验风险最小化（ERM），同时减少了因策略性行为导致分布偏移而需要的重复模型重训次数。

Conclusion: 忽略因果结构的救济策略会诱发大规模且错位的行为响应，而因果救济能够达到稳定均衡、降低博弈激励，并在实际应用中表现更优。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse aims to provide individuals with actionable changes to improve their predicted outcomes in high-stakes classification settings, such as loan and mortgage applications. However, most existing approaches focus only on flipping a model's prediction, without accounting for whether the recommended changes lead to genuine improvement in an individual's true qualifications or merely enable strategic gaming of the classifier. Consequently, deployed recourse policies can induce behavioral responses that degrade predictive accuracy and become ineffective after model retraining.
  In this work, we formalize this failure mode through a causal performative framework for recourse. We model how recourse actions propagate through a structural causal model, capturing interactions among features as well as their effect on the true label. These causal responses induce a non-convex optimization problem, even under standard convex losses. We characterize conditions under which performatively stable solutions exist and can be efficiently computed via simple iterative dynamics. Our analysis reveals that recourse policies that ignore causal structure can induce large, misaligned behavioral responses, whereas causal recourse leads to stable equilibria that reduce incentives for gaming. Experiments on both semi-synthetic and real credit datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms standard empirical risk minimization while reducing the need for repeated model retraining to accommodate distribution shifts caused by strategic agent behavior.

</details>


### [111] [Same Graph Cross-Task Transfer in GNNs: Protocols and Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28525)
*Neelam Akula,Surbhi Kumar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了同构图上的节点分类（NC）与链路预测（LP）跨任务迁移问题，提出无泄漏的评估协议，发现迁移具有强方向性：NC→LP在同质性图上稳定有益，而LP→NC仅在结构主导场景下可靠，并引入CoTask Score（CTS）来量化联合任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的图数据往往同时支持多个预测任务（NC和LP），理论上可以通过共享表示实现监督信号复用，但现有评估方法因数据划分不兼容、观测图假设不一致以及负采样规则不同，导致关于同图跨任务迁移的结论不可靠。

Method: 形式化定义了同图NC-LP迁移问题，提出无泄漏评估协议（固定节点和边划分、使用排除被评估边的共享消息传递图、LP使用固定负样本）；在三种骨干网络（GCN、GraphSAGE、GPS）上进行系统实验，并提出CTS分数来综合评估共享编码器的联合任务价值。

Result: 迁移具有强方向性：NC→LP在同质性图上始终有益；LP→NC在朴素表示复用下表现脆弱甚至可能降低精度，仅在LP容易而NC未饱和的结构主导场景下才可靠正向迁移；同质性等简单数据集统计量可用于指导迁移机制选择并避免负迁移。

Conclusion: NC和LP之间的迁移效用高度依赖于图结构和任务性质，同质性是最关键的预测因子；通过合适的协议和机制选择，可以安全地利用跨任务监督，但需要避免追求单一的通用迁移策略。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs support multiple predictive tasks over the same underlying structure, creating an opportunity to reuse supervision across node classification (NC) and link prediction (LP). However, existing evaluations often rely on incompatible splits, observed-graph assumptions, and negative sampling rules, making conclusions about same-graph cross-task transfer unreliable. We formalize same-graph NC-LP transfer and propose a leakage-free protocol that fixes node and edge splits, uses a shared message-passing graph that excludes evaluated edges, and employs fixed negatives for LP. Across three backbones (GCN, GraphSAGE, GPS), we find that transfer is strongly directional and predictable: NC $\to$ LP is consistently beneficial on homophilic graphs, while LP $\to$ NC is fragile and can even degrade accuracy under naive representation reuse. LP $\to$ NC becomes reliably positive mainly in a structure-dominant regime where LP is easy but NC is unsaturated, suggesting that LP acts as structural pretraining. Finally, we introduce the CoTask Score (CTS) to summarize joint NC+LP utility when a shared encoder must serve both tasks, and show that simple dataset statistics, especially homophily, can guide mechanism choice and help avoid negative transfer.

</details>


### [112] [APO: Unsupervised Atomic Policy Optimization for 3D Structure Prediction of Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28553)
*Shentong Mo,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出原子策略优化（APO），一种完全无监督的3D原子结构对齐框架，通过双奖励机制（潜在结构模式强化和热力学稳定性约束）消除对真实参考结构标签的依赖，在晶体和抗体结构预测上超越全监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配模型（如FlowDPO）依赖与真实坐标的监督偏好学习进行对齐，但获取新晶体相或从头设计蛋白质的实验标签成本极高，严重制约了数据稀缺场景下的结构建模。

Method: APO将组相对策略优化（GRPO）适配到3D原子环境，提出双奖励机制：(1) 通过样本相似性的特征分解来强化策略的主导潜在结构模式；(2) 施加热力学稳定性约束。框架利用无监督方式在采样组内识别物理上合理的构型，实现模型的自我纠错。

Result: 在晶体和抗体结构预测的广泛基准上，APO始终优于全监督基线，在匹配率和结构保真度上取得了新的最优结果；同时有效拉直了概率路径，显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，相较于有噪声的监督坐标匹配，内在的物理一致性可以作为更优越的对齐指导信号。

Abstract: Predicting the 3D structures of atomic systems is fundamental to advancing material science and drug discovery. While flow-matching models (, FlowDPO) have recently shown promise in this domain, their performance relies heavily on alignment with ground-truth coordinates via supervised preference learning. However, obtaining experimental labels for novel crystal phases or de novo proteins is prohibitively expensive, creating a bottleneck for structural modeling in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we propose (Atomic Policy Optimization), a fully unsupervised alignment framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth reference structures. APO adapts group-relative policy optimization to 3D atomic environments, utilizing a novel dual-reward mechanism: (i) a that reinforces the policy's dominant latent structural modes through eigen-decomposition of sample similarities, and (ii) a that enforces thermodynamic stability. Our framework enables the model to ``self-correct'' by identifying physically plausible configurations within sampled groups. Extensive benchmarks on crystal and antibody structure prediction demonstrate that APO consistently outperforms fully supervised baselines, achieving a new state-of-the-art in match rates and structural fidelity. Furthermore, we show that APO effectively straightens probability paths, significantly improving inference efficiency. Our results suggest that intrinsic physical consistency can serve as a superior guide for alignment compared to noisy, supervised coordinate matching.

</details>


### [113] [$β$-OPSD: Deriving with Policy Optimization, Training with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28582)
*Jiawei Xu,Minghui Liu,Juzheng Zhang,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出β-OPSD，将vanilla OPSD中固定的KL惩罚系数β扩展为可控的正则化参数，通过将最优策略闭式解转化为蒸馏目标（混合参考策略与教师模型的token-level logits），以低成本蒸馏近似昂贵策略优化的效果，在数学推理基准上优于原方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD虽能提升推理模型的推理能力，但在实际应用中较为脆弱，需要大量工程调试。作者发现其结构根源在于vanilla OPSD恰好是β=1的特例，KL惩罚项被隐式固定，缺乏可调的正则化自由度。

Method: 将OPSD推广为β-OPSD，β可控地权衡参考策略约束与特权教师指导；推导最优策略为参考策略与教师之间的几何插值；不直接优化高成本高方差的RL目标，而是利用闭式解构造蒸馏目标，通过混合token-level logits实现每个β对应的插值目标；结合return-to-go信用分配使token更新对齐序列级目标。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，β-OPSD一致优于vanilla OPSD，显著改善了优化稳定性和下游推理性能。

Conclusion: β-OPSD提供了从自蒸馏到策略优化之间的一条原理性通路，无需牺牲OPSD的实用性效率即可获得更好的性能与稳定性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising approach to improve reasoning language models, but it remains brittle in practice: making it work reliably often requires substantial engineering effort. We identify a structural source of this difficulty: vanilla OPSD is precisely the $β=1$ member of a broader policy-optimization family, where $β$ weights the KL penalty anchoring the student to a reference policy. This equivalence turns $β$ from an implicit value fixed at one into a controllable regularization parameter, yielding a more general formulation that trades off proximity to a reference policy against privileged teacher guidance. We introduce $β$-OPSD and derive its optimal policy as a geometric interpolation between the reference policy and the privileged teacher. Directly optimizing this objective with reinforcement learning, however, would be costly and high-variance. Rather than optimize the RL objective directly, we turn its closed-form solution into a distillation target. Each value of $β$ selects a target along the reference-to-teacher path, which we implement efficiently by mixing their token-level logits. In this way, inexpensive distillation approximates the solution of expensive policy optimization. Return-to-go credit assignment further aligns token updates with the sequence-level objective while retaining the simplicity of OPSD. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that $β$-OPSD consistently outperforms vanilla OPSD, improving optimization stability and downstream reasoning performance. Our results provide a principled route from self-distillation to policy optimization and back without sacrificing the efficiency that makes OPSD practical.

</details>


### [114] [KAISEN: Reproducible Subgroup Fairness Auditing for Clinical Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28608)
*Sparsh Roy,Samuel Girmachew,Nishita Chavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KAISEN，一个包含五个阶段（子组分层、差异测量、机制诊断、事后缓解、漂移监测）的临床风险模型审计管线，并在16个疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴和3个预先指定交叉组的合成基准上对其进行压力测试。研究发现：显著性统计与最小可检测效应相关；阈值优化可稳定降低差异但Platt缩放效果不稳；机制诊断在代理误设定下会静默失败；CUSUM阈值在不同队列间无法迁移。所有结果基于合成数据，不构成临床有效性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险模型在总体表现上可能很强，但不同患者子组的错误率差异显著。现有审计管线虽被提出用于捕捉这类问题，但其各组件很少经过压力测试，导致无法确定审计的哪些部分在什么条件下是可信的。本文旨在系统性地评估审计管线的可靠性和失败边界。

Method: 本文构建了KAISEN五阶段审计管线，涵盖子组分层、差异测量、机制诊断、事后缓解和漂移监测，并设计了一个合成基准测试平台（16个疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴、3个预先指定的交叉组），通过已知ground truth的系统性压力测试来评估每个阶段到其失败点为止的表现。

Result: 四项关键发现：(i) 显著性计数与各轴的原始EOD差距的秩相关为0.56，标准化最小可检测效应后升至0.78；(ii) 组间阈值优化在48/48次运行中稳定降低EOD（平均差值-0.285），而组间Platt缩放对EOD的影响如同抛硬币（仅19/48次改善，均值接近零）；(iii) 机制诊断在144/144个受控案例中正确分类，但在代理误设定下48个模型驱动案例全部无法识别且无失败信号；(iv) CUSUM的误报和漏报主要受队列实现（种子）影响而非疾病本身，参考阈值下27个误报和8个漏报中的7个来自不同种子（卡方p=0.002），说明阈值跨队列不可迁移。

Conclusion: 审计管线各组件在不同条件下表现差异显著：某些组件（如阈值优化）可靠，某些组件（如Platt缩放）不可靠但可通过报告方差而非均值来改进建议；机制诊断在代理误设定下存在静默失败风险；漂移监测阈值缺乏跨队列泛化性。作者强调所有结果均为合成数据且不构成临床有效性，但提供了完整可复现的代码和工件，为审计方法的信任边界提供了重要的实证参考。

Abstract: Clinical risk models routinely achieve strong aggregate performance while producing materially different error rates across patient subgroups. Audit pipelines have been proposed to catch this, but their components are rarely stress-tested, so it is unclear which parts of an audit can be trusted and under what conditions. We present KAISEN, a five-phase audit pipeline covering subgroup stratification, disparity measurement, mechanism diagnostics, post-hoc mitigation, and drift monitoring, evaluated to the point of failure on a synthetic benchmark of 16 disease tasks, 15 social-determinant axes from Healthy People 2030, and three prespecified intersections. Four findings follow. (i) Significance tracks each axis's gap against its own minimum detectable effect: rank correlation between significance count and raw equalized-odds difference (EOD) across the 15 axes is rho = 0.56, rising to rho = 0.78 once EOD is standardized by that floor. (ii) Per-group threshold optimization reduces EOD in 48 of 48 held-out runs (paired delta = -0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]), while group-wise Platt scaling -- the better calibrator -- behaves as a coin flip on EOD (19 of 48 runs improved, 95% CI [0.26, 0.55]) with mean effect near zero, so what an audit should report is the variance, not the average. (iii) The mechanism diagnostic classifies 144 of 144 controlled cases correctly but recovers none of 48 model-driven cases under proxy misspecification, with no signal that it failed. (iv) CUSUM failures and false alarms track cohort realization far more than disease: at the reference threshold, all 27 false alarms and 7 of 8 missed shifts come from different seeds (chi-squared p = 0.002), so a threshold tuned on one cohort fails to transfer. All results are synthetic with known ground truth and do not establish clinical validity. Code, artifacts, and scripts reproducing every number are released.

</details>

### [115] [AgenticER: the next frontier in Entity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27435)
*George Papadakis,Panos Korovesis,Manolis Koubarakis,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的实体解析（ER）范式——Agentic ER，将ER从被动的一次性相似度计算转变为由自主智能体执行的序贯决策过程，智能体能够主动规划策略、获取外部证据、决定是否查询额外来源或人类，并在准确性、成本和延迟之间进行优化权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ER方法（规则、深度学习、LLM）均采用被动范式，通过静态一次性相似度计算检测重复项，无法捕捉真实世界ER任务中固有的不确定性和上下文依赖性，尤其在包含流式内容、异构格式的数据湖场景中难以有效处理歧义。

Method: 作者将Agentic ER形式化为决策理论问题，提出了参考架构，识别了核心研究挑战，并概述了针对智能体行为的新型评估维度。该方法将ER重新定义为由自主智能体执行的序贯决策过程，智能体可主动规划策略、迭代收集证据、跨多个来源推理并选择性引入人工参与。

Result: 论文形式化了Agentic ER问题，提出了参考架构，识别了核心研究挑战，并提出了面向智能体行为的新型评估维度，为数据管理与智能体交叉领域建立了新的研究方向。

Conclusion: 通过引入Agentic ER，作者旨在推动实体解析从被动范式向主动智能体范式的转变，建立数据管理与智能体交叉的新研究方向，以更好地应对真实世界中复杂、不确定且依赖上下文的实体解析挑战。

Abstract: Entity Resolution (ER) is a fundamental problem in data management, playing a critical role in tasks like data cleaning and knowledge graph construction. The existing ER approaches range from traditional rule-based to deep learning techniques and LLM-based methods, but typically operate under a ``passive paradigm'', as duplicates are detected through static, one-shot similarity computations. Such approaches fail to capture the inherently uncertain and context-dependent nature of real-world ER tasks, especially in data lakes with streaming content in heterogeneous formats such as CSV files, JSON files, RDF dumps, and free text. In such settings, resolving ambiguity often requires iterative evidence gathering, reasoning across multiple sources, even selective human involvement. To cover this gap, we advocate a paradigm shift from passive to Agentic ER, which frames ER as a sequential decision-making process that is performed by autonomous agents. These agents actively plan ER strategies, acquire external evidence, decide when to query additional sources or humans, and optimize trade-offs between accuracy, cost, and latency. We formalize Agentic ER as a decision-theoretic problem, we propose a reference architecture, we identify core research challenges, and outline new evaluation dimensions tailored to agentic behavior. By introducing Agentic ER, we aim to establish a new research direction at the intersection of data management and intelligent agents.

</details>


### [116] [Fully Inductive Cardinality Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28311)
*Tim Schwabe,Lukas Ketzer,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FICE是首个完全归纳式的学习型基数估计器，通过双GNN架构（编码器+解码器）对SPARQL BGP查询进行基数估计，无需针对新知识图谱或变化图重训练，在10个KG上将中位数q-error从13.54降至5.34。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型基数估计器虽然优于基于统计和采样的方法，但它们都是转导式的（transductive），当知识图谱发生变化或应用于新图谱时需要进行重训练，这严重阻碍了其在实际三元组存储系统中的部署应用。

Method: FICE采用双耦合GNN组件：1）编码器GNN在KG的因子图视图上进行消息传递，生成实体和关系嵌入，并通过理论证明BGP基数仅取决于绑定项2跳邻域内的局部函数，从而支持局部消息传递；2）解码器GNN沿着查询的连接拓扑组合嵌入以预测对数基数。编码器和解码器联合训练，使用邻域采样扩展到百万级三元组图谱，并将嵌入生成与基数解码解耦以实现亚毫秒级延迟。

Result: 在10个知识图谱上与学习型和非学习型基线方法对比，FICE将整体中位数q-error从最佳竞争者的13.54降至5.34，并在尾部行为上全面优于所有方法。

Conclusion: FICE证明了完全归纳式的学习型基数估计是可行的，能够泛化到完全未见过的图谱（包括未见过的关系），无需重训练即可部署，显著提升了基数估计的准确性，为学习型估计器在实际三元组存储中的落地铺平了道路。

Abstract: Query optimization of Basic Graph Patterns (BGP) SPARQL queries over Knowledge Graphs (KG) requires accurate cardinality estimation. Recently published learned estimators outperform statistics- and sampling-based approaches, but share a limitation preventing their adoption in real-world triplestores: they are transductive and require retraining when the underlying graph changes or when applied to new graphs. We present FICE (Fully Inductive Cardinality Estimation), the first learned cardinality estimator for BGP queries over KGs that generalizes to entirely unseen graphs (including unseen relations), without any retraining. FICE is a graph neural network (GNN) with two coupled components. First, an encoder GNN over a factor-graph view of the KG produces entity and relation embeddings. We prove that BGP cardinality is a local function of the 2-hop neighborhood around bound terms in this view, motivating the local message-passing encoder. A decoder GNN then composes these embeddings along the join topology of the query to predict log-cardinality. The encoder and decoder are trained jointly, making the embeddings specialized for cardinality estimation. FICE is trained using neighborhood sampling to scale to KGs with millions of triples, and decouples embedding generation from cardinality decoding to enable estimation latency below a millisecond. Compared to learned and non-learned baselines over 10 KGs, FICE reduces the overall median q-error from 13.54 (for the best competitor) to 5.34 and dominates all approaches in tail behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [ESBT: A Scalable and Deterministic Sequence CRDT for Distributed Collaborative Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28101)
*Moulay Driss Mechaoui,Abdessamad Imine*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出扩展Stern-Brocot树（ESBT），一种用于分布式协同文本编辑的标识符分配方案，通过稠密、确定性且紧凑的标识符空间来约束标识符增长，在保持强最终一致性的同时显著提升性能和降低内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列CRDT虽然保证强最终一致性，但在长时间高并发编辑场景下存在标识符无限增长和内存消耗不断增加的问题，严重限制了系统的可扩展性和性能。

Method: 基于数学上扎实的Stern-Brocot树扩展，设计一种稠密、确定性且紧凑的标识符分配方案（ESBT），用于约束标识符增长，同时保持确定性排序和强最终一致性。

Result: 在50个协同站点、高达100,000个并发操作的工作负载下，纯插入场景响应性提升28-88%，混合插入/删除场景提升59-74%；标识符内存消耗在开头和随机插入模式下降低50-75%。在对抗性中间插入工作负载（10,000操作）下，响应性提升86.53%，标识符大小减少92.81%。

Conclusion: ESBT有效解决了现有序列CRDT的主要可扩展性限制，为大规模协同编辑系统提供了高效且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Modern collaborative editing systems require efficient mechanisms for managing concurrent updates across distributed replicas. Sequence Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) have become the de facto standard for supporting decentralized collaboration; they enable decentralized replicas to apply operations in arbitrary order while converging to a common state. Although existing sequence CRDTs guarantee Strong Eventual Consistency (SEC), they often suffer from uncontrolled identifier growth and increasing memory consumption during long-running, highly concurrent editing sessions, which severely limits their scalability and performance. This paper presents the Extended Stern-Brocot Tree (ESBT), a mathematically grounded identifier allocation scheme for distributed collaborative text editing, providing a dense, deterministic, and compact identifier space. The proposed allocation strategy bounds identifier growth while preserving deterministic ordering and Strong Eventual Consistency. Experimental evaluation using workloads of up to 100,000 concurrent operations generated across 50 collaborating sites shows that ESBT improves responsiveness by 28 to 88% under pure insertions and 59 to 74% under mixed insertion/deletion workloads, while reducing identifier memory consumption by 50 to 75% in beginning and random insertion patterns compared with the best-performing baseline sequence CRDTs (Logoot and LSEQ). Under the adversarial middle-insertion workload (10,000 operations), ESBT further improves responsiveness by 86.53% and reduces identifier size by 92.81%. These results demonstrate that ESBT effectively addresses the principal scalability limitations of existing sequence CRDTs and provides an efficient and scalable foundation for large-scale collaborative editing systems.

</details>


### [118] [SmartGen: Seamless Disaggregated LLM Inference with Selective KV Cache Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28150)
*Xuchuan Luo,Jiacheng Shen,Xin Wang,Yangfan Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SmartGen是一个针对分离式LLM推理（prefill和decoding分离部署）的KV缓存传输引擎，通过选择性传输关键KV缓存条目来缓解自建部署中租用云实例的跨节点网络带宽瓶颈，相比全量KV缓存传输可将首令牌时间（TTFT）降低高达4.3倍，同时保持相当的后续解码性能和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流LLM推理系统广泛采用prefill和decoding阶段分离的架构，但该架构在自托管、租用云实例的场景下面临严重挑战：分离节点之间传输海量KV缓存会轻易饱和有限的跨节点网络带宽，成为系统性能瓶颈。

Method: SmartGen设计了三路数据传输路径来实现选择性KV缓存传输：1）基于profile的主动传输路径，在prefill阶段识别并推送关键KV缓存条目到decoding节点；2）并行按需传输路径，在decoding阶段同时获取远程和本地KV缓存条目；3）投机性传输路径，最终将所有KV缓存交付到decoding节点。同时解决两个核心挑战：prefill阶段的精准KV选择和decoding阶段的高效KV获取。

Result: 实验结果表明，与典型的全量KV缓存传输方法相比，SmartGen将time-to-second-token（TTST）降低最多4.3倍，同时提供可比的后续解码性能和准确性。

Conclusion: SmartGen通过选择性KV缓存传输有效缓解了分离式LLM推理中的网络带宽瓶颈，在不牺牲解码性能和准确性的前提下显著提升TTST，为自托管LLM部署在受限网络环境下的可行性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Disaggregating the prefill and decoding stages of large language model (LLM) inference into two separate sets of nodes is widely adopted in today's LLM serving systems. However, such an architecture poses significant challenges for self-hosted LLM deployments on rented cloud instances, since transferring enormous key-value (KV) caches between disaggregated nodes can easily saturate the limited inter-node network bandwidth. In this paper, we propose to mitigate the network bottleneck by selectively transferring essential KV cache entries across the two stages. There are two challenges to achieve selective KV cache transfer, i.e., accurate KV selection during the prefill stage, and efficient KV fetching during the decoding stage. To address these challenges, we design SmartGen, a KV cache transfer engine that allows seamless disaggregated LLM inference with three data transfer paths. Specifically, we leverage 1) a profile-based proactive transfer path to identify and push essential KV cache entries to the decoding node during the prefill stage, 2) a parallel on-demand transfer path to simultaneously fetch remote and local KV cache entries during the decoding stage, and 3) a speculative transfer path to finally deliver all KV caches to the decoding node. Experimental results show that SmartGen reduces time-to-second-token by up to 4.3x compared with the typical full KV cache transfer approach while offering comparable subsequent decoding performance and accuracy.

</details>


### [119] [A Cloud Continuum Research Infrastructure for Distributed CPS Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28193)
*Fabio Orazio Mirto,Giuseppe Tricomi,Luca D'Agati,Andrea Sabbioni,Stefano Silvestri,Francesco Longo,Giovanni Merlino,Armir Bujari,Paolo Bellavista,Antonio Puliafito*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于SLICES Cloud Continuum Blueprint的两层参考架构，用于云计算连续体（Edge-Fog-Cloud）实验环境，将研究基础设施层与应用层分离，并通过两个用例（可再生能源社区管理和AirWatch监控）在40次系统化运行中验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算连续体应用需要能够结合异构边缘、雾、云和高性能计算资源的实验环境，同时需要保证可重复性、可观测性以及对分布式部署的控制能力，现有方案缺乏支持多种连续体应用的多领域参考架构。

Method: 提出两层参考架构：研究基础设施层负责暴露和管理分布式资源，应用层按Edge-Fog-Cloud模式组织网络物理工作流，将放置、时序和数据溯源作为一等实验关注点。通过物理与虚拟化边缘部署的对比实验验证。

Result: 通过可再生能源社区管理和AirWatch两个代表性用例，在地理分布式基础设施上进行了40次系统化运行，比较了虚拟化和物理边缘部署的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该架构能够支持多种连续体应用而非单一领域原型，使研究者能够在同一可编程基础设施上部署、定制和比较不同的控制和监控策略。

Abstract: Cloud Continuum applications require experimental environments capable of combining heterogeneous Edge, Fog, Cloud, and high-performance computing resources while preserving reproducibility, observability, and control over distributed deployments. This paper presents a two-level reference architecture for Cloud Continuum experimentation built on top of the SLICES Cloud Continuum Blueprint. The proposed approach separates the research-infrastructure layer, which exposes and manages distributed resources, from the application layer, where Cyber-Physical workflows are organized according to an Edge-Fog-Cloud pattern in which placement, timing, and data provenance are treated as first-class experimental concerns.
  The architecture is designed to support multiple continuum applications rather than a single domain-specific prototype. At the Edge, applications interact with physical devices and perform low-latency sensing or safety actions; at the Fog, they execute near-source coordination, mediation, and stream-processing logic; at the Cloud, they consolidate global knowledge through analytics, optimization, and visualization. This partitioning enables researchers to deploy, customize, and compare alternative control and monitoring strategies over the same programmable infrastructure substrate.
  The approach is validated through two representative use cases: Renewable Energy Community management, where distributed Digital Twin coordination and time-window-based energy control are requested, and AirWatch, a monitoring pipeline focused on anomaly detection, low-latency alerting, and cloud-side aggregation. Both workloads are evaluated through a systematic campaign of 40 runs comparing virtualized and physical edge deployments over a geographically distributed infrastructure.

</details>


### [120] [Queue-Theoretic Admission Control for Multi-Tenant GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28223)
*Sohan Kunkerkar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文形式化了GPU集群的作业准入问题，将其建模为多类别、多资源排队网络，证明了待处理队列可分解为有界等待时间的工作负载和需要重构的无界工作负载两类，并验证了该模型在Kueue系统中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU集群运维人员无法预测待处理工作负载需要等待多长时间才能被准入，现有的贪心启发式方法没有形式化的等待时间保证。

Method: 将GPU集群准入形式化为多类别、多资源排队网络，证明结构分解定理；对有界等待的工作负载，将每个集群队列建模为M/G/k系统，通过向量装箱归约确定有效服务器数k；通过从向量装箱的归约证明最优准入排序是NP难的。

Result: 在Kueue（Kubernetes标准工作负载排队系统）上使用CPU、内存和GPU资源进行了验证：向量k_eff能正确识别瓶颈资源维度，Little定律精确成立，Erlang-C近似在保守方向上持续高估观察到的等待时间。

Conclusion: 该工作为GPU集群准入提供了形式化的等待时间保证框架，证明了最优准入的NP难特性，同时验证了模型在实际系统中的准确性和保守性。

Abstract: GPU cluster operators cannot predict how long pending workloads will wait for admission. Existing systems use greedy heuristics with no formal wait time guarantees. We formalize GPU cluster admission as a multi-class, multi-resource queueing network and prove a structural decomposition: the pending queue partitions into quotable workloads (bounded wait time under stability) and unfeasible workloads (no finite bound without reconfiguration). For quotable workloads, we model each cluster queue as an M/G/k system where the effective server count k is determined by a vector packing reduction; under an explicit stochastic domination assumption, we establish O(1/(1-rho)) wait time scaling. We prove that optimal admission ordering is NP-hard under multi-dimensional resource demands via reduction from vector bin packing. We validate on Kueue, the standard Kubernetes workload queuing system, using CPU, memory, and GPU (via Dynamic Resource Allocation) resources. The vector k_eff correctly identifies bottleneck resource dimensions, Little's Law holds exactly, and the Erlang-C approximation consistently overestimates observed wait times in the conservative direction.

</details>


### [121] [FAIR-Compute: A Roadmap for Fair and Efficient Allocation of Federated Digital Research Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28290)
*Konstantinos,E. Zachariadis,Ahmed Sayed,Dimitris Fotakis,Angeliki Mathioudaki,Wan Shuen Siaw*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究从算法博弈论、交通经济学与高性能计算调度三个交叉视角，系统研究了联合数字研究基础设施（DRI）中稀缺计算资源的分配问题。研究发现：分配记录衡量的是占用而非利用；简单透明的调度启发式算法经调优后可接近离线全信息基准；未管理的联邦化会产生类似Braess悖论的负载集中问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着英国对高性能计算、高吞吐计算和数据存储的需求不断增长，稀缺计算资源的分配方式——而不仅仅是资源总量——已成为决定英国科研生产力的关键因素。研究旨在解决联合数字研究基础设施中"谁运行、何时运行、依据什么证据运行"这一核心政策问题。

Method: 项目采用三种证据链：一是英国及国际分配实践的景观综述（涵盖EuroHPC、WLCG、ACCESS、JASMIN和DiRAC），辅以利益相关者调查；二是基于策略性、不确定性用户报告的机制设计模型；三是基于公开Fresco/Anvil工作负载轨迹和受控合成压力测试的仿真研究。

Result: 三个关键发现：第一，分配记录衡量的是资源占用而非实际利用，系统无法回答资源是否产生生产性价值这一关键问题；第二，经过调优的简单透明调度启发式算法性能接近离线全信息基准，近期机会在于调优和仪器化现有调度器而非替换；第三，联邦化虽然确实有价值，但在不加管理时表现为无协调路由，可能实现最小平均延迟却将负载集中到接收系统上，形成类似Braess悖论的效果。

Conclusion: 调度器设置一旦需要由共享系统决定谁运行、何时运行及依据什么证据，就不再是纯粹的技术问题，而是政策问题。研究建议近期应聚焦于调优和监测现有调度器，并需要对联邦化进行主动管理以避免Braess悖论式的系统性能恶化。

Abstract: As demand for high-performance computing (HPC), high-throughput computing and data storage grows, the way scarce compute is allocated -- not just how much exists -- has become a decisive factor in the productivity of UK research. FAIR-Compute studies allocation in a federated Digital Research Infrastructure (DRI) as a problem at the intersection of algorithmic game theory, transport economics and HPC scheduling. Our central observation is simple: once a shared system must decide who runs, when and under what evidence, its scheduler settings cease to be a purely technical matter and become policy.
  The project combined three strands of evidence: a landscape review of UK and international allocation practice (including EuroHPC, WLCG, ACCESS, JASMIN and DiRAC) supported by a stakeholder survey; a mechanism-design model of allocation under strategic and uncertain user reports; and a simulation study built on the public Fresco/Anvil workload trace and controlled synthetic stress tests. Three results recur across all three strands. First, allocation records measure occupancy (resources reserved) rather than utilisation (useful work done), so the system cannot currently answer the question UKRI and DSIT most want answered -- whether resources deliver productive value. Second, simple, transparent scheduling heuristics perform close to an offline full-information benchmark once tuned, so the near-term opportunity is to tune and instrument existing schedulers rather than replace them. Third, federation is genuinely valuable but behaves like uncoordinated routing when left unmanaged: it can minimise average delay while quietly concentrating load -- and potentially harm -- on the receiving system. This mirrors a classic effect from road-traffic economics (Braess's paradox), where letting everyone independently pick the fastest route can leave the whole network worse off.

</details>


### [122] [A Taxonomy of Performance Metrics for the Distributed Computing Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28407)
*Praveen Kumar Donta,Boris Sedlak,Alfreds Lapkovskis,Alaa Saleh,Ying Li,Victor Casamayor Pujol,Ilir Murturi,Manuel Otero Barbasan,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式计算连续体系统（DCCS）提出了一个结构化的性能指标分类体系（taxonomy），将指标分为计算层、网络层和应用/用户层三大类，同时强调了可持续性、可观测性、适应性、数据局部性、迁移感知和连续体碎片化等新兴维度，并给出了数学公式及指标获取需求（范围、阶段和方法）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DCCS性能评估实践往往只关注单一维度（如计算、网络、能效或应用质量），无法提供跨层的全面视图。随着AI和大数据应用在数据中心与边缘环境之间广泛分布，需要一种透明且一致的性能评估方法。

Method: 提出了一个结构化的DCCS性能指标分类体系（taxonomy），按计算级、网络级和应用/用户级对指标进行归类，补充了新兴维度（如可持续性、可观测性、适应性等），提供了数学公式说明，并总结了指标获取的三个维度的需求：获取范围（单节点/多节点/全系统）、获取阶段和测量方法。

Result: 形成一个覆盖多维度、多层级的DCCS性能指标体系，并明确了各指标的数学定义及其在异构动态连续体环境中的适用性；同时阐明了指标获取的约束条件，区分了适用于运维监控还是实验评估的指标。

Conclusion: 该分类体系有助于研究者与实践者对DCCS进行更全面、一致的性能评估，弥补了现有评估实践中孤立维度的不足，为跨层系统行为的理解和优化提供了结构性框架。

Abstract: Performance evaluation is essential for understanding, comparing, and improving computing systems, including Distributed Computing Continuum Systems (DCCS). In recent years, computational requirements have changed substantially with the growth of artificial intelligence and large-scale data-driven applications. These application tasks are increasingly distributed between resource-intensive data centers and resource-constrained edge environments. In this context, novel computing continuum architectures and algorithms are emerging, creating a need for transparent and consistent performance evaluation. However, existing evaluation practices often focus on isolated dimensions, such as computation, networking, energy efficiency, or application-level quality, and therefore provide only a partial view of cross-layer DCCS behavior. This paper presents a structured taxonomy of performance metrics for DCCS. The taxonomy organizes metrics into computing-level, network-level, and application/user-level categories, while also highlighting emerging dimensions such as sustainability, observability, adaptability, data locality, migration awareness, and continuum fragmentation. Further, we provide mathematical formulations and discuss their relevance to heterogeneous and dynamic continuum environments. We also summarize metric acquisition requirements in terms of acquisition scope, acquisition phase, and measurement method. These requirements help clarify whether a metric can be collected from a single node, multiple nodes, or the full system, and whether it is more suitable for operational monitoring or experimental evaluation.

</details>


### [123] [Safe Quotes for Retroactive Liquidity Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28522)
*Peter Bro Miltersen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了跨区块链/分片环境中锁定交换（lock-swaps）的自动做市商（AMM）定价问题。作者证明了在一般情况下计算安全报价（safe quote）的最小可能输出是一个NP难问题，即使使用任意固定乘法近似比也无法在多项式时间内求解；但提出了两个具有输入相关近似比的实用算法来规避这一障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在跨链或跨分片的自动做市商场景中，常乘积流动性池无法原子性地执行一系列交换。Aanes等人提出的锁定交换和追溯性常乘积流动性池为此提供了价格保证机制，但在存在活跃锁定时，计算安全报价的最小可能输出是一个尚未解决的计算复杂度问题，解决这一问题对于该机制的部署至关重要。

Method: 本文首先通过归约证明了在一般情况下（未解决的提供与回收流动性共同存在时），计算安全报价的最小可能输出不存在任何固定乘法近似比的伪多项式时间算法（除非P=NP）。随后，提出了两个计算安全报价的实用算法，这些算法具有依赖于输入的近似比，在实际场景中可能表现良好。

Result: 证明了除非P=NP，否则不存在多项式时间算法能在一般情况下的安全报价计算中达到任何固定乘法近似比（如50%）。同时提出了两个简单实用的算法，其近似比依赖于具体输入，在实践中有望令人满意，从而绕过了这一计算困难性障碍。

Conclusion: 锁定交换功能的部署虽然面临理论上的计算复杂度障碍（NP难且不可近似），但通过输入相关的近似算法可以在实践中获得可接受的安全报价，因此该功能仍具有实际可行性。

Abstract: Automated market makers exchange assets through liquidity pools whose quoted prices depend on their reserves, with constant product pools being the most common. When such pools reside on different blockchains or shards, a sequence of swaps cannot in general be executed atomically. Aanes et al. introduced lock-swaps and retroactive constant product liquidity pools to provide price guarantees for such a setting. A retroactive pool implicitly maintains a virtual pool for each possible execute/cancel resolution of its active locks. In the presence of active locks, serving a new swap request requires computing a safe quote; a quote with an output that does not exceed the minimum possible output, taken over all virtual pools. The quote being safe is a hard constraint ensuring the integrity of the pool. A soft constraint is to make the quote as close to the minimum possible output as possible. Aanes et al. gave a simple and efficient algorithm for computing the exact minimum when unresolved provides and reclaims of liquidity do not coexist, showed by an explicit example that the algorithm fails in general, and left the computational complexity of the general case open. In this paper, we show that unless P is equal to NP, there is no polynomial time algorithm that computes in the general case a safe quote with any fixed multiplicative approximation ratio (e.g., 50%) relative to the exact minimum. This seems like a severe obstacle for deployment of the lock-swap functionality. However, we also present two simple and practical algorithms for computing safe quotes that have input-dependent approximation ratios that are likely to be satisfactory in practice, thus circumventing that obstacle.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [OwlPath: Lossless Knowledge Compression for LLM Bug Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27249)
*Bo Zhang,Ren Pan,Huan Chen,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OwlPath，一个基于OWL2本体推理的代码结构检索层，构建在CodeGraph之上，利用tree-sitter解析将多语言代码编码为统一OWL2本体，通过传递闭包引擎和OWL软件知识图谱两个模块，实现结构感知的代码检索。在SWE-bench Pro基准上获得68.4%的严格应用率，token使用减少28.8%，运行时间减少39.5%，召回率提升2.06倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM软件工程智能体受限于有限上下文窗口（约100K token），需要存储结构相关的代码子集来解决问题。现有检索模型将代码视为纯文本，无法有效处理多跳依赖关系（如子类链、传递调用者、接口实现），导致智能体通过缓慢的试错来解决问题。

Method: OwlPath采用两个互补模块：(1) 传递闭包引擎，通过SPARQL属性路径查询一次性获取所有结构关联符号，捕获字符串匹配遗漏的多跳关系；(2) OWL软件知识图谱（OWL-SKM），预计算紧凑的3KB摘要，包含模块树、核心API和问题相关符号，引导智能体在首次查询时就定位目标模块。系统基于tree-sitter解析支持多语言仓库，将语言特定语义编码为统一OWL2本体。

Result: 在18个SWE-bench Pro实例上，OwlPath获得68.4%的严格应用率（CodeGraph基线为66.7%），token使用减少28.8%，运行时间减少39.5%。在67个实例的离线检索测试中，召回率提升2.06倍（0.464 vs 0.226），命中率达88.1%（CodeGraph为59.7%）。在37个问题的结构检索基准上，召回率从4.4%提升至28.8%，传递调用者和接口任务的准确率达69-80%。

Conclusion: OwlPath通过OWL2本体推理实现了无损知识压缩，有效克服了LLM智能体在代码结构检索中的上下文窗口限制，显著提升了多跳依赖关系的检索效率和准确性。

Abstract: LLM-based software engineering agents are constrained by limited context windows: roughly 100K tokens must store structurally relevant code  subsets to resolve bugs. Standard retrieval models treat code as plain text, forcing agents to resolve multi-hop dependencies including subclass  chains, transitive callers and interface implementations through slow trial and error. We tackle this limitation with lossless knowledge  compression, encoding source code into an OWL2 ontology to answer structural queries using minimal relevant code fragments.
  We present OwlPath, an OWL2 reasoning layer atop CodeGraph, a widely used code intelligence platform with 500K+ GitHub stars, offering a unified  CLI for structural code retrieval. Powered by tree-sitter parsing, OwlPath supports multi-language repositories (Python, JavaScript,  TypeScript, Go, etc.) and encodes language-specific semantics into a unified OWL2 ontology. It adopts two complementary modules. First, a  transitive-closure engine fetches all structurally linked symbols via single SPARQL property-path queries, capturing multi-hop relations missed  by string matching. Second, the OWL Software Knowledge Map (OWL-SKM) precomputes a compact 3KB summary with module trees, core APIs and  issue-related symbols, directing agents to target modules in the first query.
  Evaluated on 18 SWE-bench Pro instances, OwlPath obtains a 68.4% strict-apply rate versus 66.7% for the CodeGraph baseline, cutting token usage  by 28.8% and runtime by 39.5%. In offline retrieval tests over 67 instances, OwlPath improves recall 2.06 times (0.464 vs 0.226) and reaches  88.1% hit rate compared to CodeGraph's 59.7%. On a 37-question structural retrieval benchmark, recall rises from 4.4% to 28.8%, with 69-80%  accuracy on transitive caller and interface tasks.

</details>


### [125] [Do Context Files Help Coding Agents? A Two-Agent Ablation Study on Real Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27250)
*Prakhar Khatri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过受控消融实验研究了持久化上下文文件（AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md）对AI编程智能体的实际效果。实验覆盖两个前沿智能体（Claude Code和Codex）、17个真实任务、288次评估运行，发现上下文注入策略并未显著提升正确性（差异被约束在10-15个百分点以内）。原因在于智能体的失败主要源于实现技能不足，而非缺乏仓库知识。任务难度存在智能体特异性，这解释了以往研究结论的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化上下文文件已成为AI编程智能体的标准实践，但关于其有效性的证据互相矛盾。作者希望通过受控实验厘清上下文文件究竟能否提升智能体的任务完成效果，并解释此前研究结论不一致的原因。

Method: 采用受控消融设计：在两个前沿智能体（Claude Code和Codex）上注入策略进行对比，覆盖17个来自3个仓库的真实任务（15个共享+2个仅Codex），运行288次评估并使用黄金标准测试。通过等价性检验限定效果边界，进行失败模式分类，并设计操作探针验证AGENTS.md能否将接近成功转变为成功。同时计算任务难度的智能体特异性（Spearman相关系数）。

Result: 上下文注入策略在两个智能体上均未带来可测量的正确性提升（效果被等价性检验限定在10-15个百分点以内）。失败模式分析表明智能体主要败于实现技能（功能设计、模式选择、精确接线），而非缺失可通过上下文文件补充的仓库知识。操作探针证实真实AGENTS.md无法将接近成功转变为成功。任务难度在智能体间仅中度相关（Spearman rho=0.75），具有智能体特异性。

Conclusion: 当前依赖上下文文件提升智能体表现的假设缺乏实证支持；智能体的主要瓶颈在于实现能力而非知识缺失。此前单智能体研究结论矛盾的根源在于不同研究所选任务落在不同智能体的信息差异区间。作者开放了全部代码、数据和复现分析。

Abstract: Persistent context files (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md) are standard practice for guiding AI coding agents, yet evidence for their effectiveness is contradictory. We present a controlled ablation of context-injection strategy across two frontier agents (Claude Code and Codex), 17 real tasks from 3 repositories (15 shared + 2 Codex-only), and 288 evaluated runs with gold-test evaluation. Context strategy does not measurably move correctness on either agent (bounded to <=10-15pp via equivalence testing). A failure-mode triage reveals why: agents fail on implementation skill---feature design, pattern selection, exact wiring---not missing repository knowledge that a context file could supply; a manipulation probe confirms the real AGENTS.md never converts a near-miss to a pass on either agent. We further show that borderline task difficulty is agent-specific (Spearman rho=0.75), offering a candidate explanation for prior contradictions: single-agent studies draw tasks from different agents' informative bands. We release all code, data, and analysis.

</details>


### [126] [AgentS4D: Benchmarking Runtime Risks across the Execution Lifecycle of LLM-Based Workspace Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27294)
*Jiajun Zhou,Zhaoxuan Ke,Jihang Ye,Xuanze Chen,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentS4D，一个沙盒化的生命周期运行时安全评估基准，包含328个风险注入案例，在20种代理配置（4个框架×5个LLM后端）下进行了6,560次运行。研究发现68.0%的运行触发预设的不安全信号，66.22%的不安全运行仍然完成任务，表明任务完成不能证明运行时安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的工作区代理安全评估基准虽然推进了可执行安全测试和轨迹感知验证，但缺乏对风险从何处进入、如何诱导不安全行为、针对何种危害以及证据在何处出现的统一描述框架。

Method: 提出了四维运行时安全框架：六个风险来源、六种诱导策略、九种目标危害用于指导案例构建，七个生命周期检查点用于组织运行后证据。构建328个风险注入案例，在四种代理框架（Hermes、OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex）和五种LLM后端（GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、DeepSeek-V4-Pro、MiniMax-M3、Qwen3.7-Plus）的20种组合下进行全面评估。

Result: 在6,560次运行中，4,461次（68.0%）触发了预设的不安全信号。代理系统的安全性同时取决于框架-LLM配对和风险引入方式。相同诱导策略通过不同风险载体到达时安全行为显著不同；相同的目标危害通过不同载体和策略实现时响应也不同。此外，4,344次（66.22%）运行不安全但仍完成任务。

Conclusion: 任务完成不能建立运行时安全性，仅测试单一形式的风险可能掩盖重要弱点。评估应在多样化的风险条件下检查完整的代理配置，并在整个执行过程中保留证据。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based workspace agents execute stateful, multi-step workflows across heterogeneous resources, external tools, and persistent state. Their safety must therefore be assessed from actions, side effects, and state changes throughout execution. Although recent benchmarks have advanced executable safety testing and trajectory-aware verification, they rarely provide a unified account of where risks enter, how they elicit unsafe behavior, which harms they target, and where supporting evidence appears during execution. We introduce AgentS4D, a sandboxed benchmark for lifecycle-wide runtime safety evaluation.
