Its four-dimensional runtime-safety framework uses six risk-entry sources, six induction strategies, and nine target harms to guide case construction, while seven lifecycle checkpoints organize post-run evidence. AgentS4D contains 328 risk-injected cases. We evaluate all 20 combinations of four harnesses (Hermes, OpenClaw, Claude Code, and Codex) and five LLM backends (GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, MiniMax-M3, and Qwen3.7-Plus) on these cases, yielding 6,560 runs. Overall, 4,461 runs (68.0%) trigger prespecified unsafe signals. Across the 20 configurations, the observed safety of an agent system varies with both its harness-LLM pairing and how risk is introduced. Agent systems exhibit markedly different safety behavior when the same induction strategy reaches them through different risk carriers. They also respond differently to the same target harm when it is realized through different carriers and strategies. Moreover, 4,344 runs (66.22% overall) are unsafe yet complete. Thus, task completion cannot establish runtime safety, and testing only one form of a risk can conceal important weaknesses. Evaluations should examine complete agent configurations across diverse risk conditions and retain evidence throughout execution.

</details>


### [127] [A Taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27302)
*Anastasia Mavridou,Hazel M. Taylor,Sandy Lozito,Louise A. Dennis,Michael Fisher,Marie Farrell*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机团队协作(HRT)需求分类体系。该体系基于对361条跨领域需求的分析，构建了包含6个高层类别和21个低层子类别的两级层次化分类框架，并通过专家评估和独立语料库验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统越来越多地部署在安全关键和任务关键领域，人与机器人需要协作完成复杂任务。然而，现有人机团队协作需求分散在不同的来源中，缺乏一个统一的框架来应对人机协作任务的复杂性。

Method: 从14个跨领域来源（学术文献、工业文献、标准和监管指南）中提取了361条需求作为构建语料库，通过迭代分类和细化，开发出包含6个高层类别（信息提供、关系控制、决策支持、安全机制、性能监控、基础系统能力）和21个低层子类别的两级层次化分类体系。通过5位领域专家评估进行验证，并在独立汇编的448条需求（来自19个来源、覆盖6个HRT领域）上进行了效用演示。

Result: 成功开发出两级层次化HRT需求分类体系，包含6个高层类别和21个低层子类别。通过5位领域专家的评估以及独立语料库（448条需求、19个来源、6个HRT领域）的效用演示，验证了该分类体系的完整性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该分类体系填补了人机团队协作需求缺乏统一框架的空白，为跨领域的人机协作需求提供了结构化的分类方法，对自主系统在安全关键领域的部署具有重要的指导意义。

Abstract: Autonomous systems are increasingly deployed in safety- and mission-critical domains where humans and robots must operate as a team to complete complex tasks. Existing requirements for Human-Robot teamwork remain fragmented across disparate sources, with no unified framework that addresses complexities of collaborative Human-Robot tasks. We address this gap by presenting a taxonomy of Human-Robot Teamwork (HRT) requirements derived from analysis of (academic and industrial) literature, standards and regulatory guidance. We extracted a construction corpus of 361 requirements from 14 cross-domain sources. Through iterative classification and refinement, we develop a two-level hierarchical taxonomy comprising 6 high-level categories and 21 low-level subcategories that distinguish information provision, relational control, decision support, safety mechanisms, performance monitoring, and foundational system capabilities. We validate the taxonomy through expert evaluation with 5 domain specialists and a utility demonstration on an independently assembled corpus of 448 requirements drawn from 19 sources spanning six HRT domains.

</details>


### [128] [SIGIL: Compiling Agent Skills into Typed Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27309)
*Jayanaka Dantanarayana,Savini Kashmira,Lingjia Tang,Jason Mars*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出技能编译（Skill Compilation）方法，通过SIGIL系统将散文式技能文件编译为可执行harness，利用类型化的agentic中间表示AG-IR分离模型认知与代码机制，相比纯散文代理将程序步骤执行率从56%提升至86%，完整程序执行频率提升2.3倍，且消耗更少token，同时提供模型无关的可靠性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI集成代理通过散文式技能文件获取能力，但技能只被描述给运行时而非编码其中，导致模型每次都需要重新推导控制流，可能跳过强制验证步骤。现有解决方案（手工编写harness）虽然有效但繁琐，且舍弃了散文技能成功的编写界面。因此需要一种自动化的编译方法。

Method: 提出Skill Compilation方法，在SIGIL系统中实现。核心是AG-IR，一种类型化的agentic中间表示，将模型拥有的认知（cognition）与代码拥有的机制（mechanism）分离。通过将散文技能编译为可执行harness，使程序结构固化在代码中。

Result: 在30个技能和两代模型的实验中：编译后的harness执行86%的强制步骤（散文代理仅56%），完整程序执行频率提升2.3倍，token消耗降至0.58倍。关键的是，编译harness在跨两代模型时保持86%的稳定表现，而散文代理则在56%至68%之间波动。

Conclusion: 技能编译有效解决了散文代理步骤执行率低和模型依赖性的问题，通过AG-IR分离认知与机制，既保留了散文技能的易用编写界面，又获得了harness的可靠性保障，且该保障具有模型无关性。

Abstract: AI-Integrated agents increasingly acquire capability from skills: prose procedure files loaded into a model's context and run by a tool-calling loop. A skill is described to the runtime but never encoded in it, so the model re-derives its control flow on every run and may skip mandated verification. Across 30 skills and two model generations, a prose agent performs only 56% of the steps its own skill mandates, while producing artifacts that pass output checks. The remedy is known: write a harness, in which the procedure is program structure. However, hand-writing harnesses is tedious and discards the authoring surface that made skills succeed. To address this limitation, we introduce Skill Compilation, realized in SIGIL, which compiles a prose skill into an executable harness. At its center is AG-IR, a typed agentic intermediate representation separating model-owned cognition from code-owned mechanism. Compiled harnesses perform 86% of mandated steps, complete the full procedure 2.3x as often, and require 0.58x the tokens. Notably, the guarantee is model-independent: the harness holds at 86% across two model generations while prose swings from 56% to 68%.

</details>


### [129] [TrustChain-Review: A Risk-Adaptive Blockchain and Game-Theoretic Framework for Trustworthy AI-Assisted Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27310)
*Mohammad Naserameri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustChain-Review框架，结合区块链证据层、三方博弈论模型和风险敏感性治理规则，用于提升代码审查的可信度。通过30轮模拟实验比较六种治理配置，发现全证据配置效果最好但成本最高，而风险自适应配置在成本效率和信任度之间取得了更实用的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助软件开发加速了编码和审查流程，但也带来了责任归属困难的问题：开发者可能提交未经充分验证的代码，审查者可能仅进行有限检查就批准更改，而集中式声誉记录也难以审计。这些挑战使代码审查的可信度和问责机制面临严峻考验。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：基于区块链的证据层、涉及开发者/审查者/平台三方的博弈论模型，以及当预期收益证明成本合理时施加更强治理的规则。评估使用Diff Quality Estimation数据集进行受控模拟，对六种治理配置进行30次独立运行比较，考察声誉准确性、信任收敛、恶意审查检测、表面审查检测、平台净效用、治理成本和成本效率等指标。

Result: 全证据配置在声誉、信任和检测结果方面表现最佳，但治理成本也最高。风险自适应配置通过选择性施加更强控制来降低治理成本并提高成本效率，但信任和检测结果略低于全证据设置。

Conclusion: 强有力的基于证据的治理最适合高风险或审计敏感的更改；对于常规或低风险贡献，选择性治理在可信度和运营成本之间提供了更实用的平衡。

Abstract: Context: AI-assisted software development can speed up coding and review, but it also makes accountability harder to establish. Developers may submit insufficiently verified code, reviewers may approve changes with limited inspection, and centralized reputation records may be difficult to audit. Objectives: This study introduces TrustChain-Review, a framework that combines verifiable evidence, strategic incentives, and risk-sensitive governance to support more trustworthy code review. Methods: The framework includes a blockchain-based evidence layer, a three-player game-theoretic model for developers, reviewers, and the platform, and a rule that applies stronger governance when the expected benefit justifies its cost. The evaluation uses a controlled simulation calibrated with the Diff Quality Estimation dataset. Six governance configurations are compared over 30 independent runs using reputation accuracy, trust convergence, malicious-review detection, superficial-review detection, net platform utility, governance cost, and cost-efficiency. Results: The full-evidence configuration produces the strongest reputation, trust, and detection results, but it also has the highest governance cost. The risk-adaptive configuration lowers this cost and improves cost-efficiency by applying stronger controls selectively, although its trust and detection results are lower than those of the full-evidence setting. Conclusion: Strong evidence-based governance is most appropriate for high-risk or audit-sensitive changes. For routine or lower-risk contributions, selective governance offers a more practical balance between trustworthiness and operational cost.

</details>


### [130] [PROGRESS: Property-Guided Regression Search for Semantic Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27359)
*Davis Tocheuk Mo,Noshin Ulfat,Matthew B. Dwyer,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PROGRESS框架，将基于意图的属性测试（property-based testing）与基于搜索的进化回归测试生成相结合，通过语言模型提取意图相关的代码上下文生成可执行的jqwik属性，扩展DynaMOSA实现属性感知的适应度函数，从而既能深入程序状态又能检测违反预期行为的缺陷。在25个大型Java系统上，PROGRESS检测到58%的当前版本缺陷（回归测试生成为0），并满足70/150个难达属性的前置条件（独立jqwik仅18个）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的回归测试生成虽然能获得高结构覆盖率，但其预言（oracle）源自被测系统本身，已有缺陷会被记录为预期行为而无法暴露；基于属性的测试提供独立的语义预言，但依赖高质量属性且难以深入深层状态或满足选择性前置条件。两种方法各有局限，需要一种融合两者优势的测试生成方法。

Method: PROGRESS包含三个核心组件：(1) 提取承载意图的代码上下文，使用语言模型管道生成可执行的jqwik属性，同时限制实现信息泄漏；(2) 扩展EvoSuite的DynaMOSA算法，为每个属性增加搜索目标和属性感知的适应度函数，奖励通过前置条件的进展并优先选择能够证伪（falsify）的执行；(3) 绑定属性参数并使用jqwik提供的生成器，将量化输入与演化的测试序列关联，引导生成过程同时朝覆盖率和缺陷检测目标推进。

Result: 在25个大型Java系统上的评估表明：PROGRESS检测到328/562个当前版本缺陷（58%），而回归测试生成为0；对于150个难达属性，PROGRESS满足其中70个的所有前置条件，而独立jqwik仅满足18个。消融实验显示文档和调用方/被调用方上下文是生成有效可执行属性的关键。PROGRESS在保持结构探索能力的同时，能暴露基于回归断言遗漏的缺陷。

Conclusion: PROGRESS成功融合了覆盖引导的进化测试生成与意图驱动的属性测试，解决了单一方法无法同时兼顾结构探索深度和语义预言独立性的问题。该方法在缺陷检测能力和属性可达性上均显著优于各基线方法，证明了意图属性和属性感知搜索目标相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Search-based regression-test generation effectively explores complex program structures, yielding high structural coverage, but its oracles are derived from the system under test: faults already present are recorded as expected behavior rather than exposed. Property-based testing offers independent semantic oracles, but depends on high-quality properties and gives little guidance for reaching deep states or satisfying selective preconditions.
  We present PROGRESS (PROperty-Guided REgression Search for Semantic Falsification), integrating intent-driven properties into coverage-guided, search-based evolutionary test generation to reach deep program states and detect violations of intended behavior. PROGRESS (1) extracts intent-bearing code context and uses a language-model pipeline to generate executable jqwik properties while limiting implementation leakage; (2) extends EvoSuite's DynaMOSA with a search objective and property-aware fitness function per property, rewarding progress through preconditions and prioritizing falsifying executions; and (3) binds property parameters and uses jqwik-provided generators to connect quantified inputs to evolving test sequences, steering generation toward coverage and bug-detection goals.
  We evaluate PROGRESS on 25 large-scale Java systems against regression-test generation, standalone property-based testing, and context ablations. PROGRESS detects 328/562 current-version bugs (58%) versus none for regression-test generation, and satisfies all preconditions for 70/150 hard-to-reach properties versus 18 for standalone jqwik. Ablations show documentation and caller/callee context are key to generating valid executable properties. PROGRESS preserves structural exploration while exposing faults missed by regression-derived assertions; we release a comprehensive artifact package.

</details>


### [131] [From Backlog Items to Security Guidance: Towards Continuous Security Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27374)
*Ignacio García Núñez,Florian Angermeir,Fabiola Moyón Constante*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于NLP的待办事项（backlog）增强系统，用于在持续软件工程中检测安全相关的工作项并将其链接到相关安全需求。系统结合安全相关性分类器与基于检索增强生成（RAG）的管道，在大型受监管企业环境中开发并评估，初步结果表明该方法能帮助工程师在开发早期识别安全需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管领域的持续软件工程中，安全需求难以在待办事项中显式表达，工程师只能从简短的自由文本描述中推断安全相关性，且缺乏及时的安全需求指导。这一痛点促使作者开发自动化的backlog增强方法。

Method: 提出一个两部分的NLP系统：(1) 安全相关性分类器，用于检测安全相关的backlog项；(2) 基于检索增强生成（RAG）的四阶段管道，从安全需求文档中检索并链接相关安全需求。数据集包含288个由9位安全从业者标注的backlog项。

Result: 分类器在分布内数据上F2=0.774，在五个基准数据集上平均零样本G-measure约为0.65，匹配或超过多数已发表的经典机器学习和开源GPT基线。RAG管道检索的24个条款中有12个被评为4/5以上相关性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了初步证据表明，基于NLP的backlog增强系统可以帮助工程师在开发早期识别安全需求，从而促进持续软件工程中的主动式安全合规。

Abstract: Continuous software engineering in regulated domains requires engineering teams to address security throughout the development lifecycle. Yet making security requirements explicit in backlog items is still problematic. Engineers must instead infer security relevance of backlog items from brief, free-form descriptions and often lack timely guidance on applicable requirements. We present an NLP-based backlog enrichment system that detects security-relevant backlog items and links them to relevant security requirements. The approach combines a security-relevance classifier with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline over security requirements documents. The approach was developed and evaluated in the context of a large enterprise in highly regulated domains. We present three contributions. First, we release a dataset of 288 backlog items labeled for security relevance by nine security practitioners, with substantial agreement (Fleiss' $κ=0.787$). Second, a recall-oriented classifier achieving $F2=0.774$ in-distribution and mean zero-shot G-measure $\approx 0.65$ across five established benchmarks, matching or outperforming most published classical-ML and open-source GPT baselines. Third, we preliminarily evaluated a four-stage security requirements document-grounded RAG pipeline with two practitioners on industrial backlogs using company-internal security policies and CIS Benchmarks. Of the retrieved 24 clauses, 12 were rated at least 4/5 for relevance. Our findings provide first indicators that NLP-based product backlog enrichment can support engineers in identifying security requirements early in the development process. With this work we aim to facilitate continuous security compliance through proactive introduction of security requirements in continuous software engineering.

</details>


### [132] [SWE-NFI: Studying and Benchmarking Coding Agents for Non-Functional Improvements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27409)
*Pengyu Xue,He Yang Yuan,Xin Wang,Junkai Chen,Haonan Zhang,Boyuan Chen,Zishuo Ding,Zhenhao Li,Weiyi Shang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-NFI基准，用于评估编码智能体在功能正确性之外的非功能性改进（NFI）能力。基准包含188个来自真实开源Python项目合并请求的任务，并制定了92条可执行规则结合功能测试进行评估。实验发现，尽管最佳智能体功能正确率达70.0%，但所有智能体在整体NFI能力上均不及人类开发者，尤其在结构性代码改进方面差距显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准主要评估功能正确性，缺乏对非功能性改进（如代码质量、可维护性等）的评估支持，而真实软件开发中开发者持续进行不改变行为的质量改进，这一能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 从开源Python项目的真实合并请求中构建188个NFI任务，将开发者导向的NFI操作化为92条可执行规则，设计了结合功能正确性测试与基于规则的NFI评估的综合评估套件。

Result: 最佳商用/开源编码智能体功能正确率达70.0%，但所有智能体整体NFI能力均落后于人类开发者；在结构性代码改进方面，智能体NFI得分范围仅为0.0-1.3，而人类参考得分为1.5。

Conclusion: 当前编码智能体在功能正确性与非功能性改进之间存在显著能力差距，SWE-NFI基准为评估和推动编码智能体超越功能正确性提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Although coding agents have achieved impressive performance on correctness-oriented benchmarks, their ability to make behavior-preserving non-functional improvements (NFIs) remains underexplored. In real-world software development, developers continuously improve software quality without changing observable behavior, yet existing benchmarks primarily evaluate functional correctness and provide limited support for assessing these non-functional improvements.
  In this paper, we present SWE-NFI, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on NFIs beyond functional correctness. Our benchmark contains 188 tasks constructed from real merged pull requests in open-source Python projects. We operationalize developer-oriented NFIs into 92 executable rules and develop a comprehensive evaluation suite that combines functional correctness testing with rule-based NFI evaluation.
  We evaluate state-of-the-art commercial and open-source coding agents. Although the best-performing agent achieves a 70.0\% functional correctness rate, all evaluated agents generally fall short of human developers in overall NFI capability. The gap is particularly evident for structural code improvements, where agents' NFI scores range from 0.0 to 1.3, compared with 1.5 for the human reference. Our benchmark and findings provide a reproducible foundation for evaluating and advancing coding agents beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [133] [LimICE: Integrating LLM into ICE Framework for Efficient Loop Invariant Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27606)
*Kai Fan,ShiWen Yu,GuangSheng Fan,HaoAng Chi,WanWei Liu,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Incremental ICE框架，将IC3的增量思想融入ICE学习框架，通过定义引理级学习目标和反例过滤机制实现增量学习，并实例化工具LimICE，利用LLM生成有序引理序列，配合ICE-DT作为后备机制。在367个线性基准和50个非线性基准上，LimICE分别解决349个和47个问题，相比现有LLM基线多解决12-24%的实例且运行速度快36-63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环不变式合成是程序验证中的基础难题，由于固有不可判定性极具挑战性。现有基于机器学习的方法多采用整体式（monolithic）策略，无法严格约束学习过程，难以同时考虑所有必要条件并生成完整不变式。实际上循环不变式往往是有序的引理序列而非单一公式，这一洞察促使作者探索增量合成框架。

Method: 将IC3的增量哲学融入通用不变式学习框架ICE中。核心包括：(1) 定义引理级（lemma-specific）学习目标；(2) 引入反例过滤机制实现可靠的增量学习；(3) 实例化为工具LimICE，利用大语言模型生成有序引理序列，并整合ICE-DT作为后备机制来补充引理序列。

Result: 在367个线性基准和50个非线性基准上进行实验。LimICE在线性问题上平均15.2秒解决349个，在非线性问题上平均8.8秒解决47个。相比最先进的LLM基线，在线性和非线性基准上多解决12-24%的实例，运行速度快36-63%。同时持续优于强大的非LLM基线，在线性和非线性基准上分别多解决至少86个和27个实例。

Conclusion: 增量式学习方法能够有效克服整体式机器学习方法在循环不变式合成中的局限性，将复杂问题分解为有序引理序列是提升合成能力的关键路径。结合LLM的生成能力与经典方法的可靠性，LimICE在效率和准确率上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Loop invariant synthesis is a fundamental problem in program verification, yet the inherent undecidability makes it highly challenging. Recent studies have increasingly employed various machine learning techniques to generate loop invariants. However, most of these methods adopt a monolithic approach. Due to the inability to strictly constrain the learning process, learning-based methods struggle to simultaneously consider all necessary conditions and generate complete invariants when tackling complex problems. In fact, a loop invariant is often an ordered sequence of lemmas, rather than a single invariant formula. This motivates us to propose Incremental ICE, a novel learning framework for incremental synthesis. Our framework integrates the incremental philosophy of IC3 into the general invariant learning framework ICE. By defining a lemma-specific learning objective and introducing a counterexample filtering mechanism, we can achieve sound incremental learning. Under this framework, we instantiate a loop invariant synthesis tool, LimICE, which leverages LLMs to generate the ordered sequence of lemmas and incorporates ICE-DT as a fallback mechanism to complement the lemma sequence. Experiments on 367 linear benchmarks and 50 nonlinear benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. LimICE solves 349 (out of 367) linear problems on an average of 15.2 seconds and 47 (out of 50) nonlinear problems on an average of 8.8 seconds. Compared to the state-of-the-art LLM-based baseline, our approach solves 12-24% more instances while running 36-63% faster across linear and nonlinear benchmarks. LimICE also consistently outperforms strong non-LLM baselines and solves at least 86 and 27 additional instances on the linear and nonlinear benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [134] [Not as Sweet by Another Name: An Empirical Study of Format Robustness in LLM Document Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27648)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Xianyun Cheng,Tianlin Li,Yuwei Zheng,Yue Yang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个面向格式感知的蜕变测试框架，用于评估端到端LLM文档工作流在不同文档格式下的鲁棒性。通过大规模实证研究（48,000次工作流执行），发现仅切换文档格式就可使准确率下降高达53.63%，并导致超过41%的实例发生决策漂移；作者还设计了轻量级缓解策略，可恢复高达44.21%的格式引发的决策漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM软件测试工作主要关注单一文本提示输入下的模型和系统鲁棒性，忽略了文档工作流（如通过文件上传界面以不同格式传递相同语义内容）在不同文档格式下是否仍能保持鲁棒行为这一关键问题。

Method: 提出一个格式感知的蜕变测试框架，包含三种蜕变关系，对端到端LLM文档工作流的格式鲁棒性进行全面评估，并设计了用户视角的轻量级缓解策略。

Result: 格式变化对LLM文档工作流构成系统性且严重的威胁：仅切换格式即可导致准确率下降高达53.63%，超过41%的实例出现决策漂移；提出的缓解策略可恢复高达44.21%的格式诱导决策漂移。

Conclusion: 文档格式并非中立的包装层，而是影响LLM软件系统可靠性的关键因素，亟需在实际高风险场景的部署中引入相应的测试和安全保障措施。

Abstract: LLM-driven software systems are rapidly evolving from plain-text conversations to document-centric end-to-end workflows, where the same semantic content can be delivered in diverse document formats (e.g., CSV) through file upload interfaces. Yet existing testing work focuses on the robustness and reliability of models and systems whose input is a single prompt string, leaving a critical question unanswered: Can these document workflows maintain robust behaviors when the same content arrives in a different document format? To fill the gap, in this paper, we propose a format-aware metamorphic testing framework with three metamorphic relations to comprehensively evaluate the format robustness of end-to-end LLM document workflows. Based on this framework, we conduct a large-scale empirical study spanning four representative LLM workflows, four real-world tasks, and four document formats, comprising a total of 48,000 workflow executions. Our findings reveal that format variation poses a systematic and serious threat. Merely switching formats can cause accuracy to drop by up to 53.63% and trigger decision drifts in over 41% of instances. We further design lightweight mitigation strategies from the users' perspective that recover up to 44.21% of format-induced decision drift without model retraining. Our study demonstrates that document format is not a neutral wrapper but a critical factor affecting the reliability of LLM software systems, calling for corresponding testing and safeguards in the deployment in real-world high-stakes scenarios.

</details>


### [135] [Can Large Language Models Resolve Real Java Merge Conflicts? An Evaluation with a Calibrated LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27674)
*Bowen Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）在解决Java合并冲突中的潜力，构建了一个仅使用推理时信号的LLM求解器代理，并提出双指标评估套件（LLM评判器+确定性结构检查）来大规模可信地评估解析质量。在292个人类标注案例上，评判器达到100%精度和64.6%召回率，LLM求解器在真实冲突上匹配开发者解法的比例约为55%，显著超越传统工具AutoMerge（36.7%），优势主要来自覆盖率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构化/半结构化合并工具经常因启发式规则不适用而放弃解决冲突，而LLM几乎可以为任何冲突生成候选解析，但大规模评估其解析质量困难，因为获取人工偏好判断无法规模化。作者希望同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 首先构建一个generate-validate-retry的LLM求解器代理，仅使用推理时信号（冲突标记、Java解析器、重复声明检查），完全不依赖开发者答案。评估采用双指标套件：(1) 基于G-Eval的LLM-as-judge开发者匹配指标，使用前先用ConflictBench人工标签校准；(2) 不使用LLM的确定性结构有效性检查。

Result: 在292个人类标注案例的元评估中，评判器达到100%精度（零误接受）和64.6%召回率。LLM求解器在约55%的真实冲突上匹配开发者自身解析（保守下限），在覆盖率公平比较下（55-59%）超出最强传统工具AutoMerge（36.7%）约18-22个百分点。优势主要来自覆盖率差异，因为传统工具在20-90%的冲突上选择弃权，而LLM在强制解析下从不弃权。此外，LLM评判器接受了5个未通过确定性结构检查的解析中的4个，说明结构正确性不能委托给LLM。

Conclusion: LLM在合并冲突解决上具有显著优势，尤其在覆盖率方面远超传统工具；经过校准的LLM评判器可提供可信的大规模评估；但结构有效性的验证必须依赖确定性检查而非LLM，两者应结合使用。

Abstract: Merge conflicts are a recurring cost of collaborative software development, and the traditional structured and semi-structured merge tools that address them frequently abstain: when their heuristics do not apply, they leave the conflict unresolved. Large language models (LLMs) can instead produce a candidate resolution for almost any conflict, but measuring whether those resolutions are actually good at scale is hard, because obtaining human desirability judgments for every model output does not scale. We study both problems together on real Java merge conflicts from ConflictBench. We first build an LLM solver as a generate-validate-retry agent that uses only inference-time signals (conflict markers, a Java parser, and duplicate-declaration checks) and never sees the developer's answer. We then evaluate its resolutions with a two-metric suite: (1) a developer-match LLM-as-judge implemented as a G-Eval metric and, crucially, calibrated against ConflictBench's human labels before use, and (2) a deterministic structural-validity check that uses no LLM. On a meta-evaluation of 292 human-labeled cases, the judge reaches 100% precision (zero false accepts) at 64.6% recall, so every acceptance is trustworthy and every downstream rate is a conservative lower bound. Under this validated judge, LLM solvers match the developer's own resolution on about 55% of true conflicts (conservative floor), and under a coverage-fair comparison the LLMs (55-59%) beat the strongest traditional tool (AutoMerge, 36.7%) by roughly 18-22 points; the edge comes almost entirely from coverage, not raw accuracy, since the tools abstain on 20-90% of conflicts while the LLM under forced resolution abstains on none. Finally, the LLM judge accepted 4 of the 5 resolutions that fail the deterministic structural check, evidence that structural correctness must not be delegated to an LLM.

</details>


### [136] [A comparative analysis of automated techniques for security bug report identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27893)
*Muhammad Laiq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对多种自动化技术进行了比较分析，用于识别安全相关的bug报告。实验评估了传统机器学习方法（逻辑回归、SVM、随机森林）和深度学习方法（GPT-5.2、BERT-base、RoBERTa、SetFit），结果显示SetFit表现最佳（F1=0.80），RoBERTa具有竞争力，而GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本设置下表现不佳。跨项目实验表明迁移学习对小数据项目有益，但可能损害具有强项目特定特征的项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 及时识别安全相关bug报告对最小化软件系统漏洞窗口至关重要，但人工筛查耗时且易错。现有自动识别技术的研究文献零散，各研究使用不同实验设置和有限基线，难以比较结果并得出可靠结论，缺乏对最佳技术的清晰指导。

Method: 使用基准数据集，对七种技术进行对比分析：三种传统机器学习方法（Logistic Regression、SVM、Random Forest）和四种深度学习方法（GPT-5.2、BERT-base、RoBERTa、SetFit）。实验涵盖零样本、少样本及跨项目迁移学习场景。

Result: SetFit实现最佳整体性能，F1分数为0.80，在四个数据集中有三个优于其他技术；RoBERTa紧随其后；Logistic Regression在特定场景下仍是强基线；GPT-5.2在零样本和少样本设置下表现均较差；跨项目迁移学习对小数据项目有提升，但对强项目特异性项目可能造成性能下降。

Conclusion: SetFit是识别安全相关bug报告最有效的技术，RoBERTa是可比的替代方案。传统ML方法在某些场景仍有价值，而GPT-5.2在此任务上表现不佳。迁移学习的适用性取决于项目的数据规模与特异性，未来研究应基于统一的基准和实验设置进行技术比较。

Abstract: Timely identification of security-related bug reports is essential to minimize the window of vulnerabilities in software systems. Manually screening incoming bug reports to identify security-related issues is time-consuming, error-prone, and non-scalable for large-scale software systems. Thus, a variety of automatic techniques, including traditional machine learning (ML) techniques and large language models, have been proposed to facilitate this task. However, the literature remains fragmented. Most studies introduce or optimize a particular technique and evaluate it against a limited set of baselines, often under different experimental setups. As a result, it is difficult to compare their results and draw reliable conclusions about the effectiveness of existing approaches, leaving researchers and practitioners without clear guidance on which techniques are most suitable for the task.
  To address this gap, we conducted a comparative analysis of several promising automated techniques to identify security-related bug reports using benchmark datasets. We evaluated Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machines, Random Forest, OpenAI's GPT-5.2, BERT-base, RoBERTa, and SetFit (a state-of-the-art few-shot learning framework).
  Our results indicate that SetFit achieves the best overall performance, achieving an F1-score of 0.80 and outperforming other techniques on three of the four datasets. RoBERTa performs competitively and approaches SetFit in some projects, while traditional ML techniques, particularly Logistic Regression, remain a strong baseline in certain contexts. In contrast, GPT-5.2 performs poorly in both zero-shot and few-shot settings. In addition, cross-project experiments demonstrate that transfer learning can improve performance for projects with limited data, but may degrade results for projects with strong project-specific characteristics.

</details>


### [137] [The Case for Vibe Modeling: A Missing Step in AI-Based Trustworthy Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27923)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Michael Mittermaier,Judith Michael*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"氛围建模"（vibe modeling）作为一种介于自然语言交互和代码生成之间的轻量级中间抽象层，并通过学生调查研究了开发者在AI辅助软件开发中对LLM输出的理解、验证、信任及氛围建模有用性的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于AI的软件开发实践过度关注直接生成代码，而忽视了能够保留人类意图并支持系统行为推理的中间表示，导致理解、验证、可追溯性和信任方面的挑战。

Method: 提出vibe modeling作为自然语言交互与代码生成之间的轻量级中间抽象，并开展学生调查研究，考察对LLM输出理解、验证努力、信任以及vibe modeling感知有用性等多个维度。

Result: 研究结果为未来在vibe modeling基础上开展可信赖和可解释的AI辅助软件工程研究提供了初步证据和方向。

Conclusion: vibe modeling有望成为提升AI辅助开发中可解释性和可信赖性的有效中间抽象手段，但需要进一步研究验证其实际效用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate software artifacts from natural language prompts. While this enables rapid prototyping and lowers the barrier to software creation, it also introduces challenges related to understanding, validation, traceability, and trust. In this paper, we argue that current AI-based development practices focus too heavily on the direct generation of code and insufficiently on intermediate representations that preserve human intent and support reasoning about system behavior. We argue for vibe modeling as a lightweight intermediate abstraction between natural language interaction and code generation. To explore its potential, we present a student survey study that examines perceptions of LLM output understanding, validation effort, trust and the perceived usefulness of vibe modeling across several AI-assisted development scenarios. Our results are intended to inform future studies for trustworthy and explainable AI-based software engineering via vibe modeling.

</details>


### [138] [Specification-Guided Synthesis of Deadlock-Free Communication Protocol Refinements with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27964)
*Yang Li,Ping Hou,Nobuko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Syntropy框架，结合多参与方会话类型（MPST）规范和大型语言模型（LLMs），用于自动合成满足行为正确性的协议细化变体，实验表明其有效性达到95.6%-99.5%，同时保持高语法正确性和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式通信协议中确保行为正确性（如避免死锁）是关键挑战，而协议细化（安全替换协议）至关重要。LLMs虽在代码生成方面表现优异，但缺乏可靠产生正确行为的机制；形式化规范方法（如MPST）虽能提供死锁自由等严格保证，却缺乏自动构造协议细化的支持。

Method: 提出Syntropy框架，将细化约束直接整合到生成过程中，以MPST规范为指导，结合LLMs进行协议细化的合成，确保生成变体满足正确性保证。

Result: 综合评估表明Syntropy在多个LLM上实现95.6%-99.5%的有效性，同时保持较高的语法正确性，并生成多样化、非平凡的细化变体。

Conclusion: Syntropy成功弥合了LLMs与形式化规范之间的差距，将MPST的严格保证融入LLM生成流程中，为协议细化合成提供了一种可靠且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Ensuring behavioural correctness in communication protocols is a central challenge in distributed software systems, as subtle inconsistencies can lead to deadlocks. In such settings, protocol refinement - the safe substitution of a protocol that preserves correctness and compatibility with other components - is essential. Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and program synthesis, yet lack mechanisms to reliably produce outputs with correct behaviour. Formal specification approaches, such as multiparty session types (MPST), offer rigorous guarantees, including deadlock freedom, but provide limited support for automatically constructing protocol refinements. In this paper, we present Syntropy, a framework for synthesising protocol refinements guided by MPST specifications and LLMs. It incorporates refinement constraints directly into the generation process, ensuring the generated variants satisfy these guarantees. Our comprehensive evaluation indicates that Syntropy achieves 95.6%-99.5% validity while maintaining high syntactic correctness, and produces diverse, non-trivial refinements across multiple LLMs.

</details>


### [139] [Advancing Awkward Arrays for High-Performance CPU and GPU Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28145)
*Ianna Osborne,Manasvi Goyal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Awkward Array Python库的GPU后端最新进展，包括基于NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries实现的CUDA内核、优化内存管理和不规则数组的分段归约算法，在保持Python编程模型不变的同时显著提升GPU吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着HL-LHC分析日益依赖加速器硬件，嵌套且变长的数据结构在GPU上难以高效执行，因为这些数据需要间接索引、分段操作和不规则内存访问。现有GPU支持不足，亟需开发针对不规则工作负载的高效后端。

Method: 基于NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries构建CUDA实现，设计优化内存管理策略，开发针对ragged数组的分段归约算法，并构建后端架构和自动化验证框架。

Result: 在保持现有Python编程模型的前提下，显著提升了GPU上不规则工作负载的吞吐量；通过与CPU、CuPy和CUDA实现的基准对比验证了性能优势。

Conclusion: Awkward Array的GPU后端为高能物理中嵌套变长数据的高效GPU加速提供了可行方案，兼顾了易用性和性能。

Abstract: Awkward Array is a Python library for representing and processing nested, variable-length data that is widely used in high-energy physics. As HL-LHC analyses increasingly rely on accelerator hardware, efficient execution of irregular workloads has become essential. While dense numerical arrays map naturally to GPUs, nested and variable-length data structures remain significantly more difficult to accelerate because they require indirect indexing, segmented operations, and irregular memory access patterns. We present recent developments in the Awkward Array GPU backend, including CUDA implementations built on NVIDIA CUDA Core Compute Libraries (CCCL), optimized memory management, and segmented reduction algorithms for ragged arrays. These developments preserve the existing Python programming model while substantially improving GPU throughput on irregular workloads. We describe the backend architecture, automated validation framework, and benchmark results comparing CPU, CuPy, and CUDA implementations.

</details>


### [140] [Integrating AI into Requirements Quality Learning in Software Engineering Education: A TPACK-Guided Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28176)
*Hansika Ekanayake Mudiyanselage,Rohan Jai Dharmaraj,Malik Abdul Sami,Zheying Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在需求工程（RE）教育中，通过TPACK框架指导集成多智能体AI工具到硕士层级的需求质量分析作业中。研究发现，结构化作业设计引导学生有选择性地使用AI作为分析与评估支持，而非自动化工具，改善了用户故事质量标准的理解，学生表现出有条件的信任和主动精炼行为，为负责任地整合AI于RE教育提供了设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件工程实践中迅速普及，但如何以教学法为基础将其融入需求工程等概念密集型课程教育中，目前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 采用TPACK框架指导设计，将多智能体AI工具集成到硕士级需求质量分析作业中；使用混合方法设计（N=100，分析72份提交），考察结构化作业设计如何塑造学生的AI使用方式、影响其对用户故事质量标准的理解以及对其AI优劣势的认知。

Result: 学生选择性使用AI工具，主要将其作为分析与评估的辅助而非自动化手段；对结构性强的质量维度（如价值表述、可测试性）的对齐改进最为明显，而可协商性维度效果不一；学生表现出有条件信任、主动精炼和更高的质量标准意识，但存在中等程度的可用性挑战。

Conclusion: TPACK引导的支架式设计能够将AI功能与教学目标和RE内容对齐，为需求工程教育中负责任地整合AI提供了设计指导。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) in software engineering (SE) practice creates a need for pedagogically grounded approaches to AI integration in SE education, especially in conceptually intensive subjects such as requirements engineering (RE). This study examines a TPACK-guided integration of a multi-agent AI tool into a master-level RE assignment on requirements quality analysis. Using a mixed-methods design (N=100; 72 submissions analysed), we examine how structured assignment design shaped students' AI use, affected their understanding of user story quality criteria, and influenced their perceptions of AI's benefits and limitations. Results show that students used the AI tool selectively, mainly as support for analysis and evaluation rather than automation. Alignment improvements were most evident for structurally concrete requirements quality dimensions, such as value articulation and testability, while negotiability showed mixed effects. Students reported conditional trust, active refinement, and increased awareness of quality criteria, alongside moderate usability challenges. The findings show that TPACK-guided scaffolding can align AI affordances with pedagogical goals and RE content, offering design guidance for responsible AI integration in RE education.

</details>


### [141] [Agentic Metaverse Services: A New As-a-Service Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28242)
*Xiaofei Xu,Quan Z. Sheng,Zhongjie Wang,Boualem Benatallah,Xiao Wang,Ruipeng Han*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了生成式人工智能（GenAI）赋能的智能体服务在元宇宙环境中的应用，提出了智能体元宇宙服务（AMServ）和元宇宙中的智能体即服务（Meta-AaaS）两种新范式，并探讨了其形式、特征、原理、典型应用及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI正在重塑数字虚拟世界，对话式聊天机器人向智能体AI的转变催生了智能体服务与Agent-as-a-Service（AaaS）的新形态。将智能体服务与元宇宙结合，可以产生面向元宇宙业务处理的智能体元宇宙服务（AMServ），这成为服务计算领域的新范式，值得系统性地梳理和展望。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法，首先概述GenAI驱动的智能体和服务的演进与新特征，揭示智能体服务在元宇宙环境中的角色与原理，然后系统阐述AMServ和Meta-AaaS的形式、特征与原则，并讨论典型应用案例及未来研究方向。

Result: 提出了AMServ和Meta-AaaS的概念框架，阐明了智能体服务在元宇宙中的运作机制，列举了典型应用实例，并指出了该领域的未来发展趋势与开放研究问题。

Conclusion: AMServ和Meta-AaaS将在AI时代为人类社会和服务业带来重大机遇，促进新兴服务产业的快速发展，具有广阔的研究与实践前景。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is reconstructing the digital virtual world, upgrading agents through enhancing their abilities in autonomous learning, multi-modal interaction, content generation, and collaborative decision-making. In particular, the shift from conversational chatbots to agentic AI, the most recent significant technical breakthrough of GenAI, has brought a new form of services, agentic services and Agent-as-a-Service (AaaS), in which the agent's abilities are encapsulated, such as perception, decision-making, execution, collaboration, and content generation, to provide the customized agent services to users. The metaverse is a virtual ecosystem for human life, work, creation, and entertainment, supported by the new generation of digital technologies. Through combining agentic services and the metaverse, an Agentic Metaverse Service, denoted as AMServ, is produced for metaverse business processing, as a new form of metaverse service. The AaaS in the metaverse environment, denoted as Meta-AaaS, as an approach to realize AMServ, has become a new paradigm of agentic services and service computing. This paper overviews the evolution and new features of agents and services empowered by GenAI, reveals the roles and principles of agentic services in the metaverse environment, presents the forms, characteristics, and principles of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS, discusses the typical application examples of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS, and finally points out the new tendencies and research directions of the AMServ and the Meta-AaaS. The AMServ and the Meta-AaaS will bring great opportunities to human society and services in the AI era, and promote the rapid development of emerging service industries in the future.

</details>


### [142] [Agentic Method for Deterministic Validation of Legacy Code Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28271)
*Andras Ferenczi,Jordan Docherty,Mariya Bessonov,Matthew Findlay,Krishna Lingamneni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Locksmith Loop"的智能体测试合成方法，用于验证COBOL到Java迁移代码的正确性。该方法通过双重运行时环境（COBOL源码与Java目标代码均配有模拟器并在通用硬件上执行），结合迭代式的智能体循环进行Witness Search（探索输入以穿透程序分支）和保持等价性的变异，最终在路由边界处识别"Locked Paragraph"以突破深层探索瓶颈。在三个案例（两个开源程序和一个内部生产级COBOL程序，430到4,114行源码）中，该方法显著超越了输入搜索平台期，达到了近乎完整的覆盖率（开源程序）和91.90%的分支覆盖率（生产级程序），所有测试用例均通过确定性等价校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统COBOL程序向Java迁移后需要进行大量测试以确保功能正确性，但这一过程常因缺乏测试数据和难以验证所有边界情况而变得复杂。现有方法在测试数据生成和边界覆盖方面存在明显不足，需要一种新颖的自动测试合成方法来应对这一挑战。

Method: 该方法名为"Locksmith Loop"：首先准备两个运行时环境——COBOL源码和生成的Java目标均被植入模拟（mocks）并在非大型机（commodity hardware）上执行；然后启动迭代式智能体循环，执行Witness Search（搜索输入模拟以穿透程序分支），随后进行保持等价性（parity-preserving）的变异操作；当达到路由边界时，分析器识别出一个"Locked Paragraph"——即阻止更深层探索的条件，并针对性地突破该瓶颈。

Result: 在三个COBOL到Java的案例研究中（两个开源程序和一个内部生产级COBOL程序，源码规模从430行到4,114行），Locksmith一致地超越了输入搜索平台期的覆盖率，两个开源程序达到了近乎完全的覆盖率，内部生产级程序达到了91.90%的分支覆盖率。所有被接受的测试用例中，生成的Java代码在确定性等价校验下均与COBOL参考代码匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了利用确定性预言机（deterministic oracle）验证智能体编码输出的新颖方法，有效解决了COBOL向Java迁移过程中的测试数据缺乏和边界覆盖不足的问题，为大规模遗留系统迁移的自动化验证提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Migration of legacy COBOL programs to Java requires extensive testing to ensure correct functionality. This effort is often complicated by the lack of test data and the difficulty of validating all corner cases. In this paper we propose a novel agentic test-synthesis method, the "Locksmith Loop," which is initiated by preparing two runtime environments: the COBOL source and the generated Java target are each instrumented with mocks and executed off-mainframe on commodity hardware, then an iterative agentic loop performs Witness Search over input mocks to penetrate program branches, followed by parity-preserving mutations. When routing boundaries are reached, an analyzer identifies a Locked Paragraph: a condition preventing deeper exploration. Across three COBOL-Java case studies, spanning two open-source programs and one internal production-like COBOL program and ranging from 430 to 4,114 source lines, Locksmith consistently improved coverage beyond input-search plateaus, reaching nearly complete coverage on the two open-source programs and 91.90% branch coverage on the internal production-like COBOL program. The generated Java matched the COBOL reference under deterministic parity checks in all accepted test cases. Through these findings we demonstrate, to the best of our knowledge, a novel approach for validating agentic coding output using a deterministic oracle.

</details>


### [143] [From Textual Requirements to Microservice Architectures - A Comprehensive Evaluation of LLM-Based Design Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28307)
*Danyllo Albuquerque,José Renan,Guillermo Rodríguez,Guillermo Rodríguez,Emanuel Dantas,Ademar França,Mirko Perkusich,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合方法实验，探索OpenAI o3大语言模型能否仅从自然语言需求中生成微服务架构。实验在Bookstore和PetClinic两个系统上，采用零样本（ZS）和少样本（FS）两种提示策略进行评估。结果显示FS提示在服务识别（F1=0.97）和通信恢复（F1=0.82）上均显著优于ZS，专家评估也认可FS生成架构的模块性和合理性。研究虽表明LLM具备需求驱动架构合成的潜力，但结论受限于模型、系统规模等特定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构现代化转型中，服务识别仍是主要人工挑战。现有分解方法多为代码中心导向，在仅有文本需求的前期设计阶段难以应用。尽管LLM技术取得进展，但缺乏关于其能否仅从自然语言需求中综合生成完整微服务架构（包括服务定义和交互关系）的实证证据。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：使用OpenAI o3模型，在零样本（ZS）和少样本（FS）两种提示条件下，分别对Bookstore和PetClinic两个系统各执行一次架构生成。评估包括两方面：(1)与参考架构进行结构化对比，使用精确率、召回率和F1分数衡量服务识别与通信恢复效果；(2)盲审专家对正确性、完整性、模块性和合理性进行评分，并综合开放式反馈。

Result: OpenAI o3在FS提示下服务识别的一致性更高（ZS的F1=0.79 vs FS的F1=0.97）。通信恢复更具挑战性：ZS产生稠密架构，高召回但低精确率（F1=0.61），FS提升一致性至F1=0.82，减少不支持的依赖关系。专家评估与定量结果一致，认为FS架构比ZS输出更模块化、连贯和合理。

Conclusion: OpenAI o3在示例提示（FS）引导下展现出从需求驱动合成微服务架构的潜力，但结果具有模型和上下文特异性，仅基于两个小型系统，不能作为跨模型的普遍性证明。

Abstract: Microservice architectures have become dominant for modernizing monolithic systems, yet identifying appropriate services remains challenging and largely manual. Existing decomposition approaches are predominantly code-centric, limiting applicability in early design stages where only textual requirements are available. Despite advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), limited empirical evidence exists on their ability to synthesize complete microservice architectures from natural-language requirements, including service definitions and inter-service interactions. This study investigates whether an LLM can bridge requirements engineering and architectural design, generating architectures solely from textual requirements and evaluating structural agreement and perceived quality of results. We conduct a mixed-method study using OpenAI o3 under zero-shot (ZS) and few-shot (FS) prompting across two systems (Bookstore, PetClinic), one execution per system/condition. Architectures are evaluated through (i) comparison with reference architectures using precision, recall, and F1-score for service identification and communication recovery, and (ii) a blinded expert assessment of correctness, completeness, modularity, and plausibility, plus open feedback synthesis. OpenAI o3 identifies services with higher agreement under FS prompting (F1 = 0.79 for ZS versus = 0.97 for FS). Communication recovery is more challenging: ZS produces dense architectures with high recall but low precision (F1 = 0.61), while FS improves agreement, reaching F1 = 0.82 and reducing unsupported dependencies. Expert evaluation corroborates these results, with FS architectures perceived as more modular, coherent, and plausible than ZS outputs. OpenAI o3 shows potential for requirements-driven synthesis when guided by exemplar prompting. Results are model- and context-specific from two small systems, not model-independent proof.

</details>


### [144] [Structural Validation of LLM-Generated Microservice Decompositions Using Source-Code Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28331)
*Daniel Silva,Renan Alves,Emanuel Dantas Filho,Ademar Sousa Neto,Mirko Perkusich,Danyllo Wagner Albuquerque,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLM（OpenAI o3）生成微服务分解方案的结构依赖保持性，提出基于静态依赖分析的自动化验证管道，并通过归一化处理控制映射覆盖差异后发现zero-shot和few-shot策略在结构遵守性上等效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体系统拆分为微服务是软件现代化中的关键活动，虽然LLM能从文本需求生成语义上合理的分解方案，但这些方案是否保持源代码中实现的结构依赖尚不明确，缺乏系统性的结构遵从事评估。

Method: 对PetClinic和Bookstore两个系统，使用OpenAI o3生成微服务分解方案，构建基于静态依赖分析的自动化验证管道，对比zero-shot和few-shot提示策略，采用依赖保持（TPD）和依赖违反（TVD）指标，并进行鲁棒性分析以控制类到服务映射覆盖率的差异。

Result: 归一化后，两种提示策略产生等效的结构遵守性，TPD值在PetClinic上为68.0%，在Bookstore上为83.3%。提示策略间的表观差异主要源于映射覆盖的方法论偏差而非真实的架构质量差异。

Conclusion: 对LLM生成的分解方案进行结构评估时，应显式控制映射覆盖率，否则提示策略间的表观差异可能反映方法论偏差而非真实的架构质量。

Abstract: Decomposing monolithic systems into microservices is a key activity in software modernization. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate semantically plausible decompositions from textual requirements, it remains unclear whether these proposals preserve the structural dependencies implemented in the source code. This paper evaluates the structural adherence of microservice decompositions generated by OpenAI o3 for the PetClinic and Bookstore systems. We propose an automated validation pipeline based on static dependency analysis and compare zero-shot and few-shot prompting using dependency preservation (TPD) and dependency violation (TVD) metrics. A robustness analysis was conducted to control for differences in class-to-service mapping coverage. After normalization, both prompting strategies produced equivalent structural adherence, achieving TPD values of 68.0% (PetClinic) and 83.3% (Bookstore). The findings demonstrate that structural evaluations of LLM-generated decompositions should explicitly control for mapping coverage, as apparent differences between prompting strategies may otherwise reflect methodological bias rather than genuine architectural quality.

</details>


### [145] [Demystifying Solana Bots: From GitHub Blueprints to On-Chain Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28424)
*Xiaoye Zheng,Yujing Chen,Minghao Wu,David Lo,Difan Xie,Daoyuan Wu,Xiaohu Yang,Zhiyuan Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Solana区块链上的机器人进行了大规模的实证研究，从586个GitHub仓库和200个链上机器人地址（超过4400万笔交易）两个互补视角出发，构建了包含15个类别、5个领域的机器人分类体系，识别出机器人实现中共享的五阶段操作流水线，并揭示了不同交易平台和资产上机器人链上交易行为的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana作为高吞吐量、低交易费用的区块链平台，容易受到机器人攻击和相关金融利用的影响（2026年1月去中心化交易所的机器人相关日交易量超过2.5亿美元）。已有研究关注系统性能、智能合约安全和特定链上现象，但缺乏对Solana机器人实际实现方式及其可观察链上执行指纹的系统性理解。

Method: 采用大规模实证研究方法，从两个互补视角收集数据：(i) 从GitHub收集586个机器人代码仓库；(ii) 识别200个Solana链上机器人地址，涉及超过4400万笔链上交易。通过分析代码实现和链上行为，推导出基于实现的机器人分类体系，并分析其操作流水线和交易行为。

Result: 研究得出了包含15个类别、5个领域（如交易操作、MEV和链上分析等）的Solana机器人实现分类体系；识别出机器人实现中共享的五阶段操作流水线；并发现了不同交易平台和资产上机器人链上交易行为的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，作者指出了未来研究方向，并为在Solana区块链上构建和运营机器人提供了建议，填补了对Solana机器人实现与链上行为系统理解的研究空白。

Abstract: Solana is an emerging blockchain platform designed for high throughput and low transaction fees, making it inexpensive to submit transactions at scale and, consequently, increasing exposure to bot spamming and related financial exploitation. Solana bots are typically off-chain software systems that operate in a competitive on-chain execution environment by constructing and submitting transactions, and the bot-related transactions on the decentralized exchanges exceed 250 million dollars in daily trading volume in January 2026. Prior studies on Solana have examined system performance, smart-contract security, and specific on-chain phenomena. However, we still lack a systematic understanding of what Solana bots implement in practice and how these implementations manifest as observable on-chain execution fingerprints. To address this gap, we performed a large-scale empirical study of Solana bots from two complementary views: (i) 586 bot repositories collected from GitHub, and (ii) 200 bot addresses on Solana, with over 44 million on-chain transactions. Our study derives an implementation-grounded taxonomy of Solana bots comprising 15 categories grouped into five domains (e.g., Trading Operations, MEV, and On-chain Analytics), identifies a largely shared five-stage operational pipeline manifested in bot implementations, and uncovers systematic variation in on-chain trading behaviors of Solana bots across diverse trading platforms and assets. Based on our findings, we highlight future research directions, and provide recommendations for building and operating bots on the Solana blockchain.

</details>


### [146] [Emerging Challenges in Threat Modeling for GenAI-Augmented Systems: A View from the Trenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28431)
*Nicolás E. Díaz Ferreyra,Manish Mahesh Kumar,Nohemí Villarreal,Pankaj Pantel,Immo Brueggemann,Riccardo Scandariato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次探索了GenAI感知的威胁建模方法在中小企业场景中的应用，通过快速文献综述选取三种方法并应用于GenAI增强系统的工业案例，发现各方法识别的威胁存在差异，且对软件供应链和以人为中心的安全风险类别的支持有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式人工智能（GenAI）在软件系统中日益普及，传统的威胁建模方法（如STRIDE）已不足以评估GenAI特有的安全风险，亟需探索适用于GenAI场景的新型威胁建模方法。

Method: 采用快速文献综述筛选相关技术，系统性地将三种选定的GenAI感知威胁建模方法应用于一个涉及GenAI增强系统的工业案例研究（中小企业环境）。

Result: 不同威胁建模方法识别的威胁存在显著差异；现有方法对某些GenAI特定风险类别的支持有限，尤其是软件供应链相关风险和以人为中心的安全问题；同时报告了从业者对这些方法在中小企业开发流程中可用性和集成的看法，包括感知的工作量和采用挑战。

Conclusion: 现有GenAI感知威胁建模方法在企业实践中仍有明显不足，特别是在供应链安全和人类因素方面需要加强，且中小企业的采用面临可用性和集成方面的实际挑战。

Abstract: Threat modeling remains a central task in secure software engineering, as it enables the identification of security issues from system architectures. As Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) becomes increasingly pervasive across software systems, traditional threat modeling methods (e.g., STRIDE) are insufficient to assess emerging GenAI-specific risks. In this work, we present the first results from an exploratory assessment of GenAI-aware threat modeling methods in a Small and Medium Enterprise (SME) setting. For this, we conducted a rapid literature review to select relevant techniques and systematically applied three shortlisted methods to an industrial case study involving a GenAI-augmented system. The results highlight differences in the threats identified by each technique and reveal limited support for certain GenAI-specific risk categories, particularly those related to software supply chains and human-centered security issues. We further report practitioners' perceptions of the usability and integration of these methods in SME development workflows, including their perceived effort and adoption challenges.

</details>


### [147] [CoGate: Confidence-Gated Co-Decoding for Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28529)
*Minghao Hu,Lannan Luo,Allen Roush,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoGate，一种基于置信度门控的协同解码方法，通过控制安全专家模型在代码生成过程中的影响力来生成更安全的代码，在多个基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成中可能产生不安全代码。现有的协同解码方法（co-decoding）在结合专家模型时未考虑其置信度，当专家模型遇到未见过的模式或分布外（OOD）上下文时，其引导可能具有误导性，影响生成代码的安全性。

Method: 提出CoGate方法，这是一种置信度门控的协同解码方法。核心思想是根据安全专家模型的置信度来控制其在协同解码过程中的影响力：当专家置信度高时增强其引导，当置信度低（如遇到OOD上下文）时降低其影响，避免误导性的安全引导。

Result: 在多个LLM后端（CodeGen、DeepSeek-Coder、Qwen-Coder、StarCoder）和多个代码生成基准（HumanEval、security suite、CWEval）上进行了评估。CoGate优于现有的协同解码方法（如CoSec+），在CWEval上Func-Sec@10指标最高提升了12.6%。

Conclusion: CoGate通过置信度门控机制有效解决了现有协同解码方法中专家模型置信度未被考虑的问题，从而提升了代码生成的安全性，并在多个基准上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Large language models are widely used for code generation, but they can also produce insecure programs due to patterns learned from their pretraining data. Decoding-time steering has become an important solution to this problem: a small expert model is combined with the target model at each step to generate more secure code, which is referred to as co-decoding. However, the acceptance rule for existing co-decoding approaches does not consider the expert model's confidence. When the security expert is unconfident due to unseen patterns or out-of-distribution (OOD) contexts, its guidance can therefore be misleading. To address the challenge, we propose CoGate, a confidence-gated co-decoding approach that controls the expert's influence on the co-decoding process based on its confidence. We implement our approach and evaluate it across multiple LLM backends (CodeGen, DeepSeek-Coder, Qwen-Coder, StarCoder) on several code generation benchmarks (HumanEval, security suite, and CWEval). Our approach outperforms existing co-decoding methods (CoSec+) across multiple benchmarks, achieving up to a 12.6% gain of Func-Sec@10 on CWEval.

</details>


### [148] [PAIChecker: Uncovering and Checking PR-Issue Misalignment in SWE-Bench-Like Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28587)
*Manyi Wang,Junjielong Xu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了SWE-bench类基准中PR-Issue配对错位问题，发现13.6%实例存在五种模式十一类场景的错位，并提出PAIChecker多智能体系统用于检测此类错位，在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上分别达到92.12%和91.67%的二元准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-bench类基准广泛用于评估LLM的问题解决能力，但其构建流程中PR-Issue配对常因大型仓库的复杂性而出现错位，影响基准数据的可靠性和评估有效性。现有方法缺乏对这一问题的系统性检测手段。

Method: 提出PAIChecker多智能体系统，采用三阶段设计：特定模式识别、跨智能体标签合成、代码级验证，实现对PR-Issue错位更准确、可泛化且渐进验证的检测。

Result: 在SWE-Gym和SWE-bench Multilingual上，PAIChecker在四种LLM骨干上均取得最佳性能，二元准确率分别达到92.12%和91.67%。

Conclusion: PAIChecker能够有效且可扩展地检测SWE-bench类基准中的PR-Issue错位问题，为未来构建更可靠的此类基准提供了可行方案。

Abstract: SWE-bench-like benchmarks are widely used for evaluating LLM's issue resolution capability. They typically follow a common construction pipeline: each PR (Pull Request) is paired with its linked issue by extracting issue references from the PR description; the issue description is used as the problem statement, and the PR patch serves as the test oracle. However, due to the inherent complexity of developing and maintaining large repositories, such PR-Issue pairings are often misaligned in practice. In this work, we systematically study SWE-bench Verified instances, finding that 13.6% exhibit misalignment across five patterns in eleven fine-grained scenarios. To enable reliable and scalable construction of those benchmarks in the future, we propose PAIChecker, a multi-agent system for checking PR-Issue misalignment in SWE-bench-like benchmarks. Specifically, PAIChecker adopts a three-phase design that combines specific pattern identification, cross-agent label synthesis, and code-level validation, thereby enabling more accurate, generalizable, and progressively verified detection. Experiments on SWE-Gym and SWE-bench Multilingual show that PAIchecker achieves the best performance across all four LLM backbones, reaching up to 92.12% and 91.67% binary accuracy, respectively.

</details>


### [149] [Change2Task: From Repository Changes to Executable Coding Agent Tasks and Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28591)
*Haomin Qi,Xingliang Wang,Xuanqi Gao,Baihui Sang,Xin Zhang,Minghua Ma,Pengfei Gao,Yu Kang,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Change2Task是一个基于仓库历史记录的系统，能将已合并的pull request转换为在健康现代版本上经过验证的编码代理任务，涵盖Bug修复、功能添加、测试生成、API迁移和安全修复五类任务。从1130个候选变更中实现79.6%的验证任务构建成功率，相比基线恢复任务多29.2%，并通过复用现代基础减少10.8%的管道开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展编码代理需要持续供应可执行数据用于训练和评估，但每个任务都需要将真实软件状态与规格、开发工具和可靠验证相结合，当前缺乏高效且可规模化的任务数据生成方法。

Method: Change2Task利用仓库历史中已合并的pull request，通过三种方式（Patch Reversal、Code Mapping、Agent Reconstruction）重构任务状态，并在健康的现代仓库版本上验证从健康基线到任务状态再到恢复状态的完整生命周期。

Result: 在五类常见编码代理任务家族中，从1130个符合条件的源代码变更实现了79.6%的验证任务构建成功率；相比基于pull request的构建基线，恢复的验证任务多29.2%；历史和重构案例的代理评估匹配结果一致性高达98.0%；复用现代基础使完整管道的测量开销减少10.8%。

Conclusion: Change2Task通过将历史变更与演进的代码对齐并自动化验证任务生命周期，能够从维护良好的环境中持续生成高质量的可执行编码代理训练与评估数据，同时降低重复环境搭建和存储开销。

Abstract: Scaling coding agents requires a continuing supply of executable data for training, benchmarking, and continuous evaluation. Each task must couple a realistic software state with a specification, development tools, and reliable verification. To expand this supply, we present Change2Task, a system grounded in repository history that converts merged pull requests into verified tasks on healthy modern revisions of the same repository. It aligns historical evidence with evolved code, reconstructs task states through Patch Reversal, Code Mapping, or Agent Reconstruction, and validates the lifecycle from a healthy base to a task state and a restored state. By deriving multiple tasks grounded in developer evidence from maintained environments, Change2Task provides executable data for coding agent training and evaluation while reducing repeated environment setup, storage, and task construction effort. We evaluate the system through five common and widely adopted coding agent task families: Bug Fix, Feature Addition, Test Generation, Application Programming Interface Migration, and Security Repair. Starting from 1,130 source changes eligible for construction, Change2Task achieves 79.6% verified task construction success across these task families. On a matched candidate set, it recovers 29.2% more verified tasks than a construction baseline based on pull requests. Historical and reconstructed cases achieve up to 98.0% matched outcome agreement under agent evaluation, while reuse of modern bases reduces measured expenditure across the complete pipeline by 10.8%.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [OneShot: Index-in-Ranking with Neural Scoring for Large-Scale Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27475)
*Ziwei Li,Shuyao Li,Xufeng Cai,Xue Zou,Yiming Ma,Huiting Lu,Wujie Yan,Zhichen Zhao,Yang Lu,Zhe Wang,Rui Luo,Zhengyu Su,Dan Zhang,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OneShot提出了一种端到端的模型内索引学习框架，将索引学习与排序目标原生对齐，突破了传统检索系统中排序与索引结构性错位的长期困境，并已在Instagram工业短视频推荐系统全面部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统的检索阶段面临排序准确性与索引效率目标的根本性错位：排序优化预测与用户行为的一致性，而索引优化物品表征的结构分组以支持快速检索。这种结构性错位限制了检索系统在亿级候选规模下的表现。

Method: 提出了OneShot框架，通过端到端的模型内索引学习将索引学习与排序目标联合对齐，并以这种联合学习为结构基础，采用超越点积瓶颈的神经评分来扩展交互建模的表达能力。

Result: OneShot已在Instagram工业短视频推荐系统全面部署，显著提升了用户日活会话、参与度和使用时长。在运营排序量级下召回率提升20%，同等召回水平下效率提升10倍。

Conclusion: OneShot验证了将索引学习与排序目标原生对齐的可行性，突破了传统检索框架中点积瓶颈对交互建模表达力的限制，为大规模工业推荐系统的检索阶段提供了新的范式。

Abstract: In modern recommendation systems, retrieval serves as a primary stage responsible for filtering billions of candidate items down to thousands prior to refined ranking. To make this massive search effective and efficient, the system relies on ranking accuracy and indexing efficiency. However, these two objectives are traditionally misaligned: while the former optimizes for the alignment between ranking predictions and user behavior, the latter optimizes for a structural grouping of item representations which enables fast search among billions of candidates. Thus, despite extensive efforts to scale up interaction modeling for retrieval, they remain fundamentally limited by the structural misalignment between the ranking objectives and the proximity-learned index. In this work, we address this long-standing dichotomy by proposing a new holistic retrieval framework, OneShot. It is an end-to-end, in-model index learning framework that natively aligns index learning with ranking objectives. Using this joint learning as a structural foundation, OneShot pushes the boundaries of retrieval expressiveness by scaling interaction modeling with neural scoring beyond the persistent dot-product bottleneck. OneShot is fully deployed in Instagram's industrial short-video recommendation system, driving significant wins in user daily sessions, engagement, and time-spent. Additionally, OneShot achieves a $20\%$ recall gain at the operational ranking volume and a 10x efficiency improvement at an equivalent recall level.

</details>


### [151] [Hierarchical Reranking for Scalable Financial RAG System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27523)
*Joohyun Lee,Sungwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Hierarchical Reranker，一个针对大规模金融文档设计的RAG框架，通过预检索优化、层次化重排序架构和长上下文管理三大创新，在FinQA、FinanceBench和ConvFinQA等多个基准上取得NDCG@20达0.7918的成绩，并在ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG挑战赛中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文档（如10-K文件、表格披露和宏观经济报告）的分析需要专家推理和大量时间，而现有RAG系统难以处理混合文本-表格结构及金融文档的庞大规模，这严重限制了检索性能和生成可靠性。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Reranker RAG框架，包含三大核心创新：(1) 预检索优化（Pre-Retrieval Optimization），通过归一化、关键词扩展和表格转换提升查询清晰度和搜索效率；(2) 层次化重排序架构（Hierarchical Reranker Architecture），通过两阶段排序机制提升检索精度；(3) 长上下文管理（Long-Context Management），通过自适应输入分区和融合保持长上下文下的推理准确性。

Result: 在FinQA、FinanceBench和ConvFinQA等多个基准上实现NDCG@20得分0.7918，展现出优越的事实一致性；在ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG挑战赛中取得第二名，验证了系统的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可部署且面向金融领域优化的RAG流水线，同时提升了金融推理的准确性和可扩展性，为自动化审计报告和量化投资分析铺平了道路。源代码将在论文被接收后于GitHub公开。

Abstract: Analyzing financial documents such as 10-K filings, tabular disclosures, and macroeconomic reports demands expert reasoning and extensive time. However, existing Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems often struggle to process hybrid text-table structures or the massive scale of financial documents. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Reranker, a RAG framework designed to improve retrieval performance and generative reliability across large-scale financial datasets. The system integrates three key innovations: Pre-Retrieval Optimization, enhancing query clarity and search efficiency through normalization, keyword expansion, and table transformation; Hierarchical Reranker Architecture, improving retrieval precision through a two-stage ranking mechanism; and Long-Context Management, preserving reasoning accuracy through adaptive input partitioning and fusion under extensive contexts. Across multiple benchmarks, including FinQA, FinanceBench, and ConvFinQA, the proposed system achieved an NDCG@20 score of 0.7918 and demonstrated superior factual consistency. Its robustness was further validated by achieving second place in the ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge. This work presents a deployable, domain-optimized RAG pipeline that enhances both the accuracy and scalability of financial reasoning, paving the way for automated audit reporting and quantitative investment analysis. The source code will be made publicly available on GitHub upon acceptance.

</details>


### [152] [Heterogeneous Ranking in Industrial-Scale Recommender Systems: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27577)
*Di Bai,Jintao Liu,Zhenwei Tang,Peifan Wu,Nada Al-Thawr,Luoshu Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HA-MoE（异构自适应多门控混合专家架构）和LENS（轻量级可观测性框架），用于解决Google Discover等异构推荐Feed中的工业级多任务排序问题，并引入了DL-AUC异构感知评估指标。离线和在线实验均验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构推荐Feed（如Google Discover）整合了来自开放网络的多样化内容（网页文章、长短视频、UGC等），不同内容类型具有不同的特征密度和用户交互模式。构建统一的排序模型以在这种异构环境中保持高性能，同时避免负迁移或多数偏差，是重要的工业挑战。

Method: 1) HA-MoE：异构自适应多门控混合专家架构，将显式异构上下文同时引入门控网络和专家表示，实现有效的专家特化且不显著增加运营开销。2) LENS：轻量级可观测性框架，提供专家特化的可解释诊断，并在持续重训练中跟踪功能异构性。3) DL-AUC：异构感知评估指标，结合全局排序性能与跨段排序正确性。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集上的离线评估显示一致优于基线模型；在线A/B测试确认了在Feed活跃度和探索指标上的增益，离线与在线结果共同验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: HA-MoE结合LENS和DL-AUC为解决工业级规模推荐系统中异构Feed的管理问题提供了有效且可部署的解决方案，能够在异构内容环境下实现高质量的统一排序。

Abstract: Heterogeneous recommendation feeds present complex challenges that extend beyond those found in highly homogeneous environments (e.g., music-only or video-only closed-ecosystem platforms). In Google Discover, a unified feed integrates diverse content sourced from the decentralized open web, including web articles, long-form and short-form videos, user-generated content (UGC), and beyond. Different content types exhibit distinct feature densities and user interaction patterns. Building a unified ranking model that sustains high performance across such heterogeneity, while avoiding negative transfer or majority bias, remains a significant industrial challenge.
  This paper presents an end-to-end case study on the industrial-scale multi-task ranking of heterogeneous feeds, grounded in real-world deployment. We introduce HA-MoE, a heterogeneity-adaptive multi-gated mixture-of-experts architecture that incorporates explicit heterogeneity context into both gating networks and expert representations. This approach enables effective specialization without significantly increasing operational overhead. To support reliable deployment, we introduce LENS, a lightweight observability framework that provides interpretable diagnostics of expert specialization and tracks this functional heterogeneity across continuous retraining. We evaluate our method using Dual-Level AUC (DL-AUC), a heterogeneity-aware evaluation metric that combines global ranking performance with cross-segment ranking correctness. Offline evaluations on a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models. Furthermore, online A/B testing confirms gains in feed activity and exploration metrics. Together, offline and online results validate the effectiveness of our approach for managing heterogeneity in industrial-scale recommender systems.

</details>


### [153] [An Exploration Graph with Continuous Refinement for Efficient Multimedia Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27623)
*Nico Hezel,Kai Uwe Barthel,Konstantin Schall,Klaus Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"连续精化探索图"(crEG)的新方法，用于在大规模多媒体数据库中快速构建紧凑的近似最近邻搜索(ANNS)图，并具备最优的搜索性能，同时支持可选的边优化算法进一步提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的ANNS方法虽然搜索速度快，但存在构建速度慢或内存占用高的问题；此外，"探索性搜索"场景（查询本身属于数据库元素）在ANNS中很少被考虑，但对推荐和探索系统至关重要。

Method: 提出crEG算法，通过连续精化方式快速构建紧凑的探索图；同时设计可选的边优化算法，两者都针对无向偶度图，保证图的连通性始终成立。

Result: 实验表明，高效的ANNS并不一定意味着在探索性搜索中也有良好表现，crEG在构建速度和搜索性能之间取得了良好的平衡。

Conclusion: crEG能够快速构建紧凑且高性能的探索图，特别适用于探索性搜索场景，填补了现有ANNS方法在该领域的空白。

Abstract: As datasets and the dimensionality of feature vectors continue to grow, Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) in large multimedia databases becomes increasingly relevant. Graph-based approaches have demonstrated to offer the best trade-off between retrieval precision and search time. Despite their ability to deliver search times several orders of magnitude faster than exact search techniques, existing methods suffer from slow constructions speeds or high memory requirements. This paper presents a "continuous refining Exploration Graph" (crEG), a novel approach for rapidly constructing a compact exploration graph with state-of-the-art search performance. Additionally, it provides the ability to enhance its effectiveness even further through an optional edge optimization algorithm. Both algorithms are specifically designed to produce and operate on undirected graphs with even degrees and guarantee graph connectivity at any time, a property particularly valuable for "exploratory search", where the query is part of the database elements. Although such queries provide an advantageous starting point for graph search algorithms, they have been rarely considered in the context of ANNS, yet are crucial for recommendation and exploration systems. Our experiments demonstrate high efficiency in ANNS does not necessarily translate to a good performance in "exploratory search".

</details>


### [154] [Dynamic Exploration Graph: A Novel Approach for Efficient Nearest Neighbor Search in Evolving Multimedia Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27640)
*Nico Hezel,Kai Uwe Barthel,Bruno Schilling,Konstantin Schall,Klaus Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了动态探索图（DEG），在静态数据集高效的探索图基础上扩展了动态数据支持。通过新颖的顶点删除算法保证图连通性，以及数据分布无关的图扩展方法，DEG在流式和在线场景下均优于现有动态图算法，同时在静态数据集上也达到与最先进方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）在图像搜索、推荐系统等应用中至关重要。虽然图算法在搜索精度和时间的平衡上表现良好，但现有方法难以有效处理数据点持续增删的动态数据集，这一缺陷限制了其在动态场景中的应用。

Method: 在连续细化的探索图（Exploration Graph）基础上扩展得到动态探索图（DEG），核心包含两项创新：一是新颖的顶点删除算法，能够保证删除顶点后的图连通性；二是与数据分布无关的图扩展方法，使图结构在数据持续变化下仍保持平衡和良好连通。

Result: 在流式和在线两种场景下的实验表明，DEG在构建时间和搜索效率上均优于现有动态图算法。此外，尽管针对动态数据集优化，DEG在静态数据集上的性能与当前最先进的方法相当。

Conclusion: DEG成功解决了动态数据集中ANNS的挑战，同时保持了静态场景下的高性能，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) represents a fundamental problem in various applications (image-search, recommendation systems). While graph-based algorithms have demonstrated a good balance between search accuracy and time, handling dynamic datasets, where data points are continuously added or removed, remains a challenge. This paper introduces the Dynamic Exploration Graph (DEG), an extension of the continuous refining Exploration Graph, which retains high search efficiency for static dataset while adding essential support for dynamic data. At the core of the DEG design are two key innovations: a novel vertex deletion algorithm which guarantees graph connectivity and a data distribution-agnostic method for graph expansion. Through these mechanisms, the DEG maintains a balanced and well-connected structure, even under continuous data alterations. Empirical experiments in both streaming and online scenarios demonstrate the superior performance of the DEG, surpassing existing dynamic graph algorithms in terms of construction time and search efficiency. Although optimized for dynamic datasets, the DEG delivers results as good as current state-of-the-art approaches for static dataset, underscoring its broad applicability.

</details>


### [155] [LoopMemGR: From Behavior Logs to Evolving Memory for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27647)
*Hui Qian,Changfa Wu,Chang Liu,Binbin Cao,Jian Wu,Yuliang Yan,Han Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LoopMemGR，一种闭环推荐经验记忆框架，用于生成式推荐。除了传统行为日志外，它还维护推荐经验日志，记录过去的推荐-反馈轨迹，并通过近期、频率和全局三个互补视图提取请求相关证据，压缩为固定数量的经验token来条件化生成骨干网络。在淘宝工业数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法采用"历史即上下文"范式，反复从行为历史重建用户偏好，却丢弃系统侧的推荐决策。这造成不对称记忆：系统记住用户做了什么，但忘记自己推荐过什么及从反馈中学到什么。因此，偏好验证信号、潜在负面证据和历史探索信息无法跨请求复用。

Method: LoopMemGR包含：1) 额外的推荐经验日志记录推荐-反馈轨迹；2) 三个互补视图提取证据：近期视图（短期交互动态）、频率视图（重复推荐模式）、全局视图（跨用户可迁移规律）；3) 将信号压缩为固定数量的经验token，在有界输入预算下条件化生成式骨干网络。

Result: 在淘宝工业数据集上的大量实验表明，闭环经验积累和多视图经验提取均有效，验证了LoopMemGR在生成式推荐中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入闭环推荐经验记忆机制，使生成式推荐系统能够复用系统侧的推荐决策和反馈信号，弥补了传统行为日志在偏好验证和探索信息上的缺失，为大规模工业推荐提供了一种更完整的记忆框架。

Abstract: Generative recommendation formulates next-item prediction as conditional autoregressive generation over discrete Semantic IDs, enabling end-to-end recommendation over large-scale item spaces. However, most existing methods follow a history-as-context paradigm that repeatedly reconstructs user preference from behavior history while discarding system-side recommendation decisions after each request. This creates an asymmetric memory: the system remembers what the user has done, but not what it has previously recommended or learned from the resulting feedback. Consequently, useful preference-validation signals, potential negative evidence, and historical exploration information cannot be directly reused across requests. To address these limitations, we propose LoopMemGR, a closed-loop recommendation experience memory framework for generative recommendation. In addition to the conventional behavior log, LoopMemGR maintains a recommendation experience log that records past recommendation--feedback trajectories. It extracts request-relevant evidence through three complementary views: the recency view captures short-term interaction dynamics, the frequency view summarizes recurring recommendation patterns, and the global view distills transferable regularities shared across users. These signals are compressed into a fixed number of experience tokens to condition the generative backbone under a bounded input budget. Extensive experiments on an industrial Taobao dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of closed-loop experience accumulation and multi-view experience extraction.

</details>


### [156] [Restoring Collaborative Signals in Semantic-ID Generative Recommendation via Personalized Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27682)
*Changjiang Han,Qingyang Li,Yaqiang Zang,Jikun Kang,Pinghua Gong,Xue Liu,Bowei He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向LLM生成式推荐模型的框架，通过个性化自然语言引导，在推理时为SID生成过程注入层级化协同信号，无需修改骨干模型或重训SID，从而在保持生成过程可控的前提下提升推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成式推荐模型将推荐转化为生成语义ID（SID）的任务，但SID的紧凑编码无法同时承载内容与协同信号，二者相互竞争且协同信号往往被压制，导致推理表现差、显式推理难以转化为正确SID，多轮训练成本高且收益有限。

Method: 提出一个由个性化自然语言引导的框架，在生成过程中动态添加层级化协同线索。该方法不直接将语言映射到SID，而是利用语言将协同模式与其受众之间的可分析连接注入生成过程，在不改动骨干模型或不重训SID的前提下，在推理时恢复SID缺失的协同信号。

Result: 在推荐准确率上取得一致性提升，相比依赖显式推理或重训练的方法，生成过程在推理时即可被协同结构所支撑，效果更稳定。

Conclusion: SID无法同时兼顾内容与协同信息的根本矛盾可以通过推理时注入协同信号来缓解，个性化自然语言提供了可靠的信号通道，使得LLM推荐模型在无需重训练的情况下获得更强的个性化生成能力。

Abstract: Making LLM-based generative recommendation models stronger and more personalized through natural language and explicit reasoning is a widely anticipated yet still unsolved goal. Such models cast recommendation as autoregressively generating an item's semantic-ID (SID), a short tuple of discrete codes, so that recommending well reduces to emitting the right SID. In this setting the model verbalizes its knowledge poorly, and text and SID tokens live in misaligned embedding spaces. Deep reasoning therefore rarely turns into a correct SID, and enabling explicit "thinking" often gives no gain or even hurts. The deeper cause is that a compact SID cannot hold content and collaborative signal at once: the two compete, and collaboration loses. Because a mis-predicted SID is a wrong recommendation, this caps accuracy directly. Costly multi-round training barely helps, and few methods try to supply the missing signal at inference time. What is missing is a reliable channel that carries collaborative signal into SID generation. We therefore propose a framework, guided by personalized natural language, that adds hierarchical collaborative cues as the model generates, without altering the backbone or retraining the SIDs. Rather than mapping language onto SIDs directly, it uses language to attach analyzable links between collaborative patterns and their audiences, restoring the collaborative signal that SIDs miss. The result is consistent gains in recommendation accuracy, grounding generation in collaborative structure at inference time rather than relying on explicit reasoning or retraining.

</details>


### [157] [Measuring Alignment With Reader Highlights Net of Position and Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27739)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个非循环的社会性高亮标注评估框架，用于评估上下文压缩方法。通过匹配被标注句子与其在相同深度和长度排名下的未标注邻居来解决两个混淆因素，并在120个网页文档上验证了语言模型重要性排名方法能保留38.4%的被标注句子（相对于邻居的19.9%），效果超过单个人类读者且与高端模型相当。同时发现经典方法（如Luhn启发式）并非无效，且作者自己之前的工作中的主张无法在该语料库上复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的上下文压缩评估依赖下游任务准确率，这实际上是用另一个模型来判断什么内容重要，存在循环论证问题。自然主义的社会性高亮标注（多人独立标记同一页面的段落）提供了非循环的参照标准，但简单的评估指标（保留被标注句子比例）存在双重混淆：标注句子倾向于出现在文档前部且长度更长，导致任何偏向早期或长句的方法都能获得高分。因此需要去除这些混淆因素才能公正评估。

Method: 1）针对每个被标注句子，在同一文档中匹配等相对深度和等文档内长度排名的未标注句子作为对照；2）在仅基于位置和长度的合成零假设数据上校准所有估计器（深度分层的单一化处理会在20-36%无效应的零数据上产生假阳性）；3）使用精确随机化检验验证显著性；4）在120个网页文档（每个至少12位独立读者）上评估多种方法，包括语言模型重要性排名、朴素截断、Luhn经典启发式等。

Result: 语言模型重要性排名保留38.4%的被标注句子，相对于匹配邻居的19.9%，富集度为+0.196（p=0.0005），且跨供应商可复现。朴素截断（位置偏置）降至+0.003。单个人类读者达到+0.182，与GPT-5.4（+0.002）无法区分且低于Claude Opus 5。Luhn的1958年启发式达到+0.088，表明经典词计数方法部分有效。作者自己先前工作中的一个主张未能在本语料库上复现。

Conclusion: 通过去除位置和长度混淆后，语言模型确实能识别出社会性高亮内容，其能力超过单个人类读者但低于最先进的Claude Opus 5。经典方法并非无效但效果较弱，提示这部分能力部分可通过简单词频统计恢复。同时强调评估框架的方法论贡献：深度-长度双重匹配、合成零校准和精确随机化检验，为上下文压缩评估提供了更为严谨的非循环基准。

Abstract: Context compression discards most of a document before a language model reads it, and is normally evaluated by downstream task accuracy - which makes another model the judge of what mattered. Naturalistic social highlighting offers a non-circular reference: many people independently marking passages on the same page. But the obvious metric, the fraction of crowd-marked sentences a compressor keeps, is confounded twice: crowd marks are front-loaded and crowd-marked sentences are longer, so any method favouring early or long sentences scores well regardless of readers. We remove both by matching each marked sentence against unmarked sentences of the same document at equal relative depth and equal within-document length rank, and we calibrate every estimator on synthetic nulls built from position and length alone - a step that matters, since depth-only stratification returns a false positive on 20-36% of nulls containing no effect. On 120 web documents (at least 12 independent readers each), a language-model importance ranking keeps 38.4% of crowd-marked sentences against 19.9% of their matched neighbours: an enrichment of +0.196 [+0.148, +0.239], at p = 0.0005 under an exact randomization test that assumes nothing about clustering, and replicated cross-vendor. Naive truncation, whose keep rule is position, correctly falls to +0.003. To give the number a scale: scored identically, on the same budget, against a crowd label recomputed to exclude them, a single human reader reaches +0.182 - indistinguishable from GPT-5.4 (+0.002 [-0.081, +0.088]) and below Claude Opus 5. Classical methods are not null - Luhn's 1958 heuristic reaches +0.088 - so reader selection is partly recoverable by counting words; conditioning additionally on lexical centrality removes only 0.010, so the agreement is not centrality. We also report that a claim in our own prior work does not reproduce on this corpus.

</details>


### [158] [A Structured Knowledge Infrastructure for Domain-Specific Data Asset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27748)
*Mengdi Chen,Yuanxin Huang,Yulin Jiang,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对企业数据分析智能体在RAG检索中命中率低（Hit@10仅19.1%）且缺乏使用知识防止指标误读的两大结构性问题，提出了一套双层解决方案，并在小红书商业广告数据仓库（5,300+张Hive表、14个领域）成功部署。方案包括三层双用途知识库和闭环刷新管道，以及图引导检索器（GGR）和场景感知排序器（SAR）。在两个100题基准上，Hit@10从19.1%提升至96.6%（+77.5个百分点），知识覆盖率从56%提升至77%，端到端延迟4.84-5.33秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据分析智能体现有体系存在两大结构性失败：一是通用RAG检索命中错误的数据资产，二是无法提供使用知识以防止指标误读。论文识别出四个根本原因（C1-C4），涵盖语义鸿沟、实体歧义、模式漂移和资产使用鸿沟，亟需系统性解决方案。

Method: 提出双层解决方案：(1) 三层双用途知识库（179篇文档、八节标注模板），同时服务于检索和生成，配以闭环刷新管道实现天级新鲜度（一次是/否审批、30秒热重载）；(2) 图引导检索器（GGR）使用2,859节点知识图谱作为候选门控并进行意图路由，实现71.6倍token缩减；场景感知排序器（SAR）应用19类实体识别和显式场景标注，利用负面知识单独贡献25个百分点的Hit@10提升。

Result: 在两个100题基准上，Hit@10从19.1%提升至96.6%（+77.5个百分点），知识覆盖率从56%提升至77%，端到端延迟保持在4.84-5.33秒。该方案已在小红书商业广告数据仓库中部署运行。

Conclusion: 该双层架构有效解决了企业数据分析智能体在数据资产检索和知识利用方面的结构性缺陷，通过知识图谱门控、场景感知排序与天级新鲜度维护的知识库刷新机制，实现了检索准确率的显著提升，同时控制了延迟成本，验证了方案的商业化可行性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Enterprise data analytics agents face two structural failures: generic RAG retrieves the wrong asset (Hit@10=19.1%) and delivers no usage knowledge to prevent metric misinterpretation---stemming from four root causes (C1--C4) ranging from semantic gap and entity ambiguity to schema drift and asset-usage gap. We present a two-layer solution deployed in the commercial advertising data warehouse at Xiaohongshu (5,300+ Hive tables, 14 domains). A three-tier dual-purpose knowledge base (179 documents, eight-section annotation template) serves both retrieval and generation, with a closed-loop refresh pipeline maintaining day-level freshness (one yes/no approval, 30s hot-reload). The Graph-Guided Retriever (GGR) uses a 2,859-node knowledge graph as a candidate gate with intent routing to deliver 71.6x token reduction. The Scene-Aware Ranker (SAR) applies 19-class entity recognition and explicit scenario annotations; negative knowledge alone contributes 25 percentage points of Hit@10 gain. On two 100-question benchmarks, Hit@10 rises from 19.1% to 96.6% (+77.5pp) and knowledge coverage from 56% to 77%, at 4.84--5.33s end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [159] [Hierarchical Latent Reasoning for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27760)
*Peiyu Hu,Siying Gu,Weihai Lu,Zhuodong Liu,Yuntian Tang,Jiahao Liang,Yiying Xie,Jiang Rong,Zhaokai Luo,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HiLaR框架，一种用于LLM推荐系统的层级潜在推理框架，通过构建时间引导的层级用户偏好表征，与LLM多层潜在推理状态对齐，并结合层级感知过程奖励进行强化优化，在四个Amazon基准数据集上优于多种强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐方法中，显式自然语言推理推理开销大，而潜在推理方法主要聚焦于生成或验证中间状态，缺乏对层级（layer-wise）偏好角色和贡献的深入刻画，导致推理过程不够透明和高效。

Method: HiLaR包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建时间引导的层级用户偏好表征（从广泛偏好到细粒度当前意图）；(2) 将层级偏好表征与LLM多层潜在推理状态进行对齐；(3) 结合最终推荐反馈和基于边际目标似然增益的层级感知过程奖励，通过强化学习优化推理轨迹。

Result: 在四个Amazon基准数据集上的实验表明，HiLaR普遍优于强序列推荐、生成式推荐和基于LLM的推荐基线。消融和敏感性分析验证了层级表征学习、潜在对齐和过程级优化的各自贡献。

Conclusion: HiLaR通过层级潜在推理与层级感知过程优化，有效提升了LLM推荐系统的性能，为潜在推理在推荐任务中的应用提供了新的视角和方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for recommendation by leveraging their semantic understanding and contextual modeling capabilities. Recent studies further introduce reasoning mechanisms to improve user preference modeling. However, explicit natural-language reasoning incurs substantial inference overhead, whereas existing latent reasoning methods mainly focus on generating or verifying intermediate states, leaving their layer-wise preference roles and contributions insufficiently characterized. We propose HiLaR, a Hierarchical Latent Reasoning framework with layer-aware reinforcement optimization for LLM-based recommendation.
